1 .agents/AGENTS.md 21 lines

   1# Project Rules - Huru SMS
   2
   3Huru SMS is a generic, Tanzania-aware question answering service for every citizen (not only students, not only legal topics). It runs over SMS (shortcode `15054`, keyword `HURU`) and a web chat.
   4
   5## Product
   6- **Generic by design**: any topic is valid. Topic detection (`App\Services\TopicClassifier`) only chooses the persona, knowledge and contacts; it never refuses a topic.
   7- **Tanzanian context first**: assume TZS, East Africa Time, Tanzanian law and institutions, Swahili as the default language. Country facts live in `App\Services\TanzaniaContext`; national contacts and knowledge live in the database and are edited in the admin panel, never hard-coded in prompts.
   8- **Honesty over confidence**: the engine must say when something should be confirmed with the responsible office and must never invent phone numbers, fees or officials. Keep this in every persona and constraint change.
   9
  10## User interface and aesthetics
  11- **No "AI"/"Bot" wording** in user-facing text, page titles or labels. Present Huru SMS as a direct guidance service. The admin panel may say "engine".
  12- **Government palette**: Forest Green (`#166534` / `#15803d`), Royal Blue (`#1e40af` / `#1d4ed8`), white/off-white (`#ffffff`, `#f8fafc`). No brown, amber, gradients or glow effects on public pages.
  13- **Light, high-readability themes** for all citizen-facing pages. Plain Inter font.
  14- **SMS visibility**: the keyword `HURU` and shortcode `15054` must stay clearly visible on the home page and the web chat login screen, and must survive any layout refactor. Values come from settings (`sms_keyword`, `sms_shortcode`).
  15- Swahili is the primary language of public pages; English appears as a secondary line where it helps.
  16
  17## Engineering
  18- All channel logic goes through `App\Services\ConversationService`; do not duplicate moderation or logging in controllers or jobs.
  19- New settings are declared in `App\Support\Settings::schema()` only; the admin form, validation and defaults derive from it.
  20- Keep the inbound SMS webhook behind `AT_WEBHOOK_SECRET` and keep TLS verification on for all outbound HTTP.
  21- Tests use SQLite in memory (`phpunit.xml`); keep migrations driver-agnostic.

3 README.md 127 lines

   1# Huru SMS
   2
   3**Huru SMS** is a digital service that lets any person in Tanzania ask a question and get a clear, useful answer on their phone, in Kiswahili or English. It works on the simplest mobile phone through SMS, and on smartphones and computers through a web chat.
   4
   5Owner and developer: **Huru Digital Co. Ltd.**, Tanzania.
   6
   7---
   8
   9## 1. What the service does
  10
  11A citizen sends a question. Huru SMS reads it, understands the topic and the language, checks where the person is, looks up verified information about Tanzania, and replies with a short, practical answer. If the person needs an office or a service near them (for example a legal aid provider in Kahama), the reply names it with its contact.
  12
  13Topics covered include:
  14
  15- Law and rights (arrest, land, marriage, inheritance, tenants, contracts)
  16- Health (danger signs, clinics, vaccination, health insurance)
  17- Farming, livestock and fishing (seasons, seeds, pests, animal vaccines)
  18- Education (school system, examinations, student loans, school subjects)
  19- Government services (NIDA identity card, birth certificate, passport, TIN, government payments, electricity)
  20- Money and business (taxes, loans, mobile money safety, registering a business)
  21- Jobs and employment (contracts, leave, dismissal, job scams)
  22- Family and community (violence, child protection, where to get help)
  23- Technology and transport (SIM cards, phone safety, traffic fines, driving licence)
  24- Any other everyday question
  25
  26## 2. How a citizen uses it
  27
  28**By SMS (no internet needed)**
  29
  301. Open the message app on any phone.
  312. Write the word **HURU** followed by the question. Example: `HURU nifanyeje kupata kitambulisho cha NIDA?`
  323. Send it to **15054**.
  334. The answer arrives as an SMS within a short time.
  34
  35Helpful SMS commands:
  36
  37| Send | What happens |
  38| --- | --- |
  39| `MSAADA` or `HELP` | A short guide to the service |
  40| `LUGHA SW` / `LUGHA EN` | Choose Kiswahili or English for replies |
  41| `JINA Asha` | Save your name |
  42| `MKOA Mwanza` | Save your region, so nearby services are suggested |
  43| `ACHA` | Stop receiving replies |
  44| `ANZA` | Start again |
  45
  46**By web chat (with internet)**
  47
  481. Open the website and press **Web Chat**.
  492. Enter a phone number. A one-time code is sent by SMS to confirm it is yours.
  503. Ask questions in the chat. Previous conversations are saved, can be searched, and each answer can be rated helpful or not helpful.
  51
  52## 3. What makes it different
  53
  54- **Built for Tanzania.** The service assumes Tanzanian law, the Tanzanian shilling, East Africa time, Tanzanian institutions (NIDA, RITA, TRA, NHIF, NECTA, HESLB and others) and the real emergency numbers (112, 114, 115, 116).
  55- **Knows where you are.** It recognises all 31 regions, common districts and well-known neighbourhoods, and uses that to point people to services close to them.
  56- **Verified knowledge first.** Answers are grounded in a knowledge base and directories maintained by the operator, not only on general internet knowledge. The service says clearly when something should be confirmed with the responsible office, and it never invents phone numbers, fees or names of officials.
  57- **Two languages, one service.** It detects whether a question is in Kiswahili or English and answers in the same language.
  58- **Works on every phone.** SMS first, web second. Replies sent by SMS are kept short to fit a few text messages.
  59- **Safe to use.** Abusive language is warned and then blocked, emergencies are recognised and the right number is given first, and people can stop the service at any time.
  60
  61## 4. What the operator can manage
  62
  63A protected administration area lets Huru Digital staff:
  64
  65- see how many questions arrive, by day, by topic and by channel;
  66- read conversations and the information used to answer them;
  67- add or edit knowledge entries (one by one or by importing a file);
  68- maintain the national directory of institutions and hotlines, and the list of local services by region and district;
  69- adjust the wording and behaviour of the service per topic and language;
  70- change settings such as reply length, language policy and safety limits;
  71- block or unblock users.
  72
  73## 5. Privacy and safety
  74
  75- Only the phone number and the conversation are stored. They are needed to continue a conversation and to let the person see their own history.
  76- The web chat requires a one-time SMS code before a phone number's history can be opened.
  77- A person's name and phone number are never sent together with the question to the language technology provider.
  78- Government payments are always described as being made through official control numbers, never cash to an officer.
  79- Full terms and a privacy policy are published on the website.
  80
  81## 6. Technology used (for the technical reader)
  82
  83| Part | Technology |
  84| --- | --- |
  85| Application | PHP 8.2 with the Laravel 12 framework |
  86| Database | MySQL 8 (SQLite for quick local testing) |
  87| SMS | Africa's Talking SMS gateway (shortcode 15054, keyword HURU) |
  88| Language technology | Google Gemini, used only to word the final reply after the service has gathered the Tanzanian context |
  89| Web pages | HTML, CSS and JavaScript; installable as a phone app (PWA) |
  90| Tests | 28 automated tests covering SMS, web chat, topic detection, location detection and the administration area |
  91
  92Main parts of the source code:
  93
  94| Folder or file | Purpose |
  95| --- | --- |
  96| `app/Services/ConversationService.php` | Handles every incoming question, commands, safety checks and logging |
  97| `app/Services/TopicClassifier.php` | Detects the topic of a question and recognises emergencies |
  98| `app/Services/TanzaniaContext.php` | Regions, districts, country facts and topic rules for Tanzania |
  99| `app/Services/KnowledgeRetriever.php` | Finds the most relevant verified knowledge entries |
 100| `app/Services/PromptEngine.php` | Combines persona, context, knowledge and contacts into instructions for the reply |
 101| `app/Services/Ai/` | Connection to the language technology provider |
 102| `app/Services/SmsService.php` | Sending SMS through Africa's Talking |
 103| `app/Http/Controllers/` | Web chat, SMS receiver, community and administration screens |
 104| `database/seeders/` | Starter knowledge, directories, personas and settings |
 105| `resources/views/` | All web pages |
 106| `tests/` | Automated tests |
 107
 108## 7. Running the application
 109
 110```bash
 111cp .env.example .env         # fill in GEMINI_API_KEY, AT_* and database details
 112composer install
 113npm install && npm run build
 114php artisan key:generate
 115php artisan migrate --seed   # creates tables, the admin account and starter data
 116php artisan serve            # open http://localhost:8000
 117php artisan queue:work       # processes SMS replies (or run the scheduler every minute)
 118php artisan huru:import-legal-aid   # imports the national legal aid provider list
 119```
 120
 121The SMS gateway must be configured to deliver incoming messages to `/api/sms/inbound?token=<AT_WEBHOOK_SECRET>`.
 122
 123Administration is at `/admin/login`.
 124
 125## 8. Ownership
 126
 127Huru SMS, its source code, its content and its documentation are the property of Huru Digital Co. Ltd. All rights reserved.

4 app/Console/Commands/FetchLegalAidProviders.php 121 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Console\Commands;
   4
   5use App\Models\LocalResource;
   6use App\Services\TanzaniaContext;
   7use Illuminate\Console\Command;
   8use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
   9use Illuminate\Support\Str;
  10
  11/**
  12 * Imports registered legal aid providers from the Mama Samia Legal Aid
  13 * Campaign site (mslac.or.tz) into local_resources as category legal_aid.
  14 */
  15class FetchLegalAidProviders extends Command
  16{
  17    protected $signature = 'huru:import-legal-aid {--pages=16 : Number of listing pages to read}';
  18
  19    protected $description = 'Import legal aid providers from mslac.or.tz into the local resources directory';
  20
  21    public function handle(TanzaniaContext $tz): int
  22    {
  23        $pages = (int) $this->option('pages');
  24        $imported = 0;
  25
  26        for ($page = 0; $page < $pages; $page++) {
  27            $url = "https://www.mslac.or.tz/en/legal-aid-providers?page={$page}";
  28            $this->line('Fetching ' . ($page + 1) . "/{$pages}: {$url}");
  29
  30            try {
  31                $response = Http::retry(3, 2000)->timeout(45)
  32                    ->withHeaders(['User-Agent' => 'HuruBot/1.0 (+https://hurudigital.co.tz)'])
  33                    ->get($url);
  34            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
  35                $this->error('Request failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
  36
  37                continue;
  38            }
  39
  40            if (! $response->successful()) {
  41                $this->error('HTTP ' . $response->status());
  42
  43                continue;
  44            }
  45
  46            $chunks = explode('class="item-columns"', $response->body());
  47            array_shift($chunks);
  48            if (empty($chunks)) {
  49                $this->warn('No provider cards found; stopping.');
  50                break;
  51            }
  52
  53            foreach ($chunks as $chunk) {
  54                preg_match('/views-field-title.*?<strong class="field-content">(.*?)<\/strong>/s', $chunk, $m);
  55                $name = isset($m[1]) ? trim(strip_tags($m[1])) : null;
  56                if (! $name) {
  57                    continue;
  58                }
  59
  60                preg_match('/views-field-field-region.*?<div class="field-content">(.*?)<\/div>/s', $chunk, $m);
  61                $regionRaw = isset($m[1]) ? trim(strip_tags($m[1])) : null;
  62
  63                preg_match('/views-field-field-address.*?<div class="field-content">(.*?)<\/div>/s', $chunk, $m);
  64                $location = isset($m[1]) ? trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', strip_tags($m[1]))) : null;
  65
  66                $region = null;
  67                $district = null;
  68                $detected = $tz->detectLocation(trim(($regionRaw ?? '') . ' ' . ($location ?? '')));
  69                if ($detected) {
  70                    $region = $detected['region'];
  71                    $district = $detected['district'];
  72                }
  73                if (! $region && $regionRaw) {
  74                    $region = Str::lower($regionRaw);
  75                }
  76                if (! $district && $location && preg_match('/wilaya\s+(?:ya\s+)?([a-z\']+)/i', $location, $dm)) {
  77                    $district = Str::lower($dm[1]);
  78                }
  79
  80                $email = null;
  81                if ($location && preg_match('/[a-z0-9._%+-]+@[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}/i', $location, $em)) {
  82                    $email = $em[0];
  83                }
  84                preg_match('/views-field-field-email.*?href="mailto:(.*?)"/s', $chunk, $em2);
  85                if (! empty($em2[1])) {
  86                    $email = trim(strip_tags($em2[1]));
  87                }
  88
  89                $phone = null;
  90                if ($location && preg_match('/(?:\+255|0)[67]\d{8}/', str_replace([' ', '-', '(', ')', '/'], '', $location), $pm)) {
  91                    $phone = $pm[0];
  92                }
  93                preg_match('/views-field-field-phone.*?<div class="field-content">(.*?)<\/div>/s', $chunk, $pm2);
  94                if (! empty($pm2[1])) {
  95                    $phone = trim(strip_tags($pm2[1]));
  96                }
  97
  98                LocalResource::query()->updateOrCreate(
  99                    ['name' => $name],
 100                    [
 101                        'category' => 'legal_aid',
 102                        'region' => $region,
 103                        'district' => $district,
 104                        'location' => $location,
 105                        'email' => $email,
 106                        'phone' => $phone,
 107                        'source' => 'mslac.or.tz',
 108                        'is_active' => true,
 109                    ]
 110                );
 111                $imported++;
 112            }
 113
 114            usleep(500000);
 115        }
 116
 117        $this->info("Done. Providers imported or updated: {$imported}");
 118
 119        return self::SUCCESS;
 120    }
 121}

5 app/Http/Controllers/Admin/AuthController.php 56 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
   4
   5use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
   6use App\Models\User;
   7use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   8use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
   9use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
  10use Illuminate\Support\Str;
  11
  12class AuthController extends Controller
  13{
  14    public function showLogin(Request $request)
  15    {
  16        if ($request->user()?->isAdmin()) {
  17            return redirect()->route('admin.dashboard');
  18        }
  19
  20        return view('admin.login');
  21    }
  22
  23    public function login(Request $request)
  24    {
  25        $data = $request->validate([
  26            'email' => 'required|email',
  27            'password' => 'required|string',
  28        ]);
  29
  30        $key = 'admin-login:' . Str::lower($data['email']) . '|' . $request->ip();
  31        if (RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($key, 5)) {
  32            return back()->withErrors(['email' => 'Too many attempts. Try again in a minute.'])->onlyInput('email');
  33        }
  34
  35        $user = User::query()->where('email', $data['email'])->first();
  36        if (! $user || ! $user->isAdmin() || ! Auth::attempt(['email' => $data['email'], 'password' => $data['password']], $request->boolean('remember'))) {
  37            RateLimiter::hit($key, 60);
  38
  39            return back()->withErrors(['email' => 'These credentials do not match an admin account.'])->onlyInput('email');
  40        }
  41
  42        RateLimiter::clear($key);
  43        $request->session()->regenerate();
  44
  45        return redirect()->intended(route('admin.dashboard'));
  46    }
  47
  48    public function logout(Request $request)
  49    {
  50        Auth::logout();
  51        $request->session()->invalidate();
  52        $request->session()->regenerateToken();
  53
  54        return redirect()->route('admin.login');
  55    }
  56}

6 app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ConversationController.php 44 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
   4
   5use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
   6use App\Models\Message;
   7use App\Models\User;
   8use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   9
  10class ConversationController extends Controller
  11{
  12    public function index(Request $request)
  13    {
  14        $query = Message::query()->with(['user', 'aiLog', 'feedback'])->latest('id');
  15
  16        if ($request->filled('q')) {
  17            $q = '%' . $request->string('q') . '%';
  18            $query->where(fn ($w) => $w->where('content', 'like', $q)->orWhereHas('user', fn ($u) => $u->where('phone_number', 'like', $q)->orWhere('name', 'like', $q)));
  19        }
  20        if ($request->filled('category')) {
  21            $query->where('category', $request->string('category'));
  22        }
  23        if ($request->filled('channel')) {
  24            $query->where('channel', $request->string('channel'));
  25        }
  26        if ($request->filled('direction')) {
  27            $query->where('direction', $request->string('direction'));
  28        }
  29        if ($request->filled('date')) {
  30            $query->whereDate('created_at', $request->string('date'));
  31        }

7 app/Http/Controllers/Admin/DashboardController.php 66 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
   4
   5use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
   6use App\Models\AiLog;
   7use App\Models\Feedback;
   8use App\Models\Message;
   9use App\Models\User;
  10use App\Services\AiService;
  11use App\Services\SmsService;
  12use App\Services\TopicClassifier;
  13use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
  14
  15class DashboardController extends Controller
  16{
  17    public function index(AiService $ai, SmsService $sms)
  18    {
  19        $today = now()->startOfDay();
  20        $week = now()->subDays(7);
  21
  22        $stats = [
  23            'users' => User::query()->where('role', User::ROLE_USER)->count(),
  24            'users_today' => User::query()->where('role', User::ROLE_USER)->where('created_at', '>=', $today)->count(),
  25            'questions_today' => Message::query()->where('direction', Message::DIRECTION_IN)->where('created_at', '>=', $today)->count(),
  26            'questions_week' => Message::query()->where('direction', Message::DIRECTION_IN)->where('created_at', '>=', $week)->count(),
  27            'sms_week' => Message::query()->where('direction', Message::DIRECTION_IN)->where('channel', 'sms')->where('created_at', '>=', $week)->count(),
  28            'web_week' => Message::query()->where('direction', Message::DIRECTION_IN)->where('channel', 'web')->where('created_at', '>=', $week)->count(),
  29            'tokens_today' => (int) AiLog::query()->where('created_at', '>=', $today)->sum('total_tokens'),
  30            'latency_avg' => (int) round((float) AiLog::query()->where('created_at', '>=', $week)->where('status', 'ok')->avg('latency_ms')),
  31            'errors_week' => AiLog::query()->where('created_at', '>=', $week)->where('status', '!=', 'ok')->count(),
  32            'banned' => User::query()->where('is_banned', true)->count(),
  33            'helpful' => Feedback::query()->where('rating', 1)->count(),
  34            'unhelpful' => Feedback::query()->where('rating', -1)->count(),
  35        ];
  36
  37        $byCategory = Message::query()
  38            ->select('category', DB::raw('count(*) as total'))
  39            ->where('direction', Message::DIRECTION_IN)
  40            ->where('created_at', '>=', $week)
  41            ->whereNotNull('category')
  42            ->groupBy('category')
  43            ->orderByDesc('total')
  44            ->get()
  45            ->map(fn ($row) => ['key' => $row->category, 'label' => TopicClassifier::label($row->category, 'en'), 'total' => (int) $row->total]);
  46
  47        $daily = Message::query()
  48            ->select(DB::raw('DATE(created_at) as day'), DB::raw('count(*) as total'))
  49            ->where('direction', Message::DIRECTION_IN)
  50            ->where('created_at', '>=', now()->subDays(13)->startOfDay())
  51            ->groupBy('day')
  52            ->orderBy('day')
  53            ->pluck('total', 'day');
  54
  55        $recent = Message::query()->with(['user', 'aiLog', 'feedback'])->latest('id')->take(25)->get();
  56
  57        $health = [
  58            'engine_configured' => $ai->isConfigured(),
  59            'sms_configured' => $sms->isConfigured(),
  60            'webhook_secret' => (bool) config('services.at.webhook_secret'),
  61            'queue' => config('queue.default'),
  62        ];
  63
  64        return view('admin.dashboard', compact('stats', 'byCategory', 'daily', 'recent', 'health'));
  65    }
  66}

8 app/Http/Controllers/Admin/KnowledgeController.php 164 lines

   3namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
   4
   5use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
   6use App\Models\KnowledgeEntry;
   7use App\Services\KnowledgeRetriever;
   8use App\Services\TopicClassifier;
   9use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  10use Illuminate\Support\Str;
  11
  12class KnowledgeController extends Controller
  13{
  14    public function index(Request $request)
  15    {
  16        $query = KnowledgeEntry::query()->latest();
  17
  18        if ($request->filled('q')) {
  19            $q = '%' . $request->string('q') . '%';
  20            $query->where(fn ($w) => $w->where('title', 'like', $q)->orWhere('content', 'like', $q));
  21        }
  22        if ($request->filled('category')) {
  23            $query->where('category', $request->string('category'));
  24        }
  25        if ($request->filled('language')) {
  26            $query->where('language', $request->string('language'));
  27        }
  28

  33    }
  34
  35    public function create()
  36    {

  38    }
  39
  40    public function store(Request $request, KnowledgeRetriever $retriever)
  41    {
  42        $data = $this->validated($request);
  43        $data['keywords'] = $this->keywords($data, $retriever);
  44        KnowledgeEntry::query()->create($data);
  45
  46        return redirect()->route('admin.knowledge.index')->with('success', 'Knowledge entry created.');
  47    }
  48
  49    public function edit(KnowledgeEntry $knowledge)
  50    {
  51        return view('admin.knowledge.form', ['entry' => $knowledge]);
  52    }
  53
  54    public function update(Request $request, KnowledgeEntry $knowledge, KnowledgeRetriever $retriever)
  55    {
  56        $data = $this->validated($request);
  57        $data['keywords'] = $this->keywords($data, $retriever);
  58        $knowledge->update($data);
  59
  60        return redirect()->route('admin.knowledge.index')->with('success', 'Knowledge entry updated.');
  61    }
  62
  63    public function toggle(KnowledgeEntry $knowledge)
  64    {
  65        $knowledge->update(['is_active' => ! $knowledge->is_active]);
  66
  67        return back()->with('success', 'Entry ' . ($knowledge->is_active ? 'activated' : 'deactivated') . '.');
  68    }
  69
  70    public function destroy(KnowledgeEntry $knowledge)
  71    {
  72        $knowledge->delete();
  73
  74        return back()->with('success', 'Knowledge entry deleted.');
  75    }
  76
  77    /**
  78     * Bulk import. CSV columns: title, content, category, language, keywords (comma separated), source, summary.
  79     * JSON: an array of objects with the same keys (keywords may be an array).
  80     */
  81    public function import(Request $request, KnowledgeRetriever $retriever)
  82    {
  83        $request->validate([
  84            'file' => 'required|file|mimes:csv,txt,json|max:5120',
  85            'default_category' => 'required|in:' . implode(',', TopicClassifier::keys()),
  86            'default_language' => 'required|in:sw,en',
  87        ]);
  88
  89        $file = $request->file('file');
  90        $ext = strtolower($file->getClientOriginalExtension());
  91        $rows = [];
  92
  93        if ($ext === 'json') {
  94            $decoded = json_decode(file_get_contents($file->getRealPath()), true);
  95            if (is_array($decoded)) {
  96                $rows = isset($decoded['title']) ? [$decoded] : $decoded;
  97            }
  98        } else {
  99            $handle = fopen($file->getRealPath(), 'r');
 100            $header = fgetcsv($handle);
 101            if ($header) {
 102                $header = array_map(fn ($h) => Str::snake(Str::lower(trim((string) $h))), $header);
 103                while (($row = fgetcsv($handle)) !== false) {
 104                    if (count(array_filter($row)) === 0) {
 105                        continue;
 106                    }
 107                    $rows[] = array_combine($header, array_pad($row, count($header), null));
 108                }
 109            }
 110            fclose($handle);
 111        }
 112
 113        $count = 0;
 114        foreach ($rows as $row) {
 115            if (empty($row['title']) || empty($row['content'])) {
 116                continue;
 117            }
 118            $category = in_array($row['category'] ?? '', TopicClassifier::keys(), true) ? $row['category'] : $request->input('default_category');
 119            $language = in_array($row['language'] ?? '', ['sw', 'en'], true) ? $row['language'] : $request->input('default_language');
 120            $keywords = $row['keywords'] ?? [];
 121            if (is_string($keywords)) {
 122                $keywords = array_filter(array_map('trim', explode(',', $keywords)));
 123            }
 124            $data = [
 125                'title' => Str::limit(trim($row['title']), 250, ''),
 126                'content' => trim($row['content']),
 127                'summary' => $row['summary'] ?? Str::limit(trim($row['content']), 160),
 128                'category' => $category,
 129                'language' => $language,
 130                'source' => $row['source'] ?? null,
 131                'is_active' => true,
 132            ];
 133            $data['keywords'] = $this->keywords($data + ['keywords_text' => implode(',', $keywords)], $retriever);
 134
 135            KnowledgeEntry::query()->updateOrCreate(['title' => $data['title'], 'language' => $language], $data);
 136            $count++;
 137        }
 138
 139        return back()->with('success', "Imported {$count} knowledge entries.");
 140    }
 141
 142    private function validated(Request $request): array
 143    {
 144        return $request->validate([
 145            'title' => 'required|string|max:250',
 146            'content' => 'required|string|max:20000',
 147            'summary' => 'nullable|string|max:500',
 148            'category' => 'required|in:' . implode(',', TopicClassifier::keys()),
 149            'language' => 'required|in:sw,en',
 150            'keywords_text' => 'nullable|string|max:1000',
 151            'source' => 'nullable|string|max:250',
 152            'is_active' => 'sometimes|boolean',
 153        ]) + ['is_active' => $request->boolean('is_active')];
 154    }
 155
 156    /** Explicit keywords plus title tokens, so retrieval works even when the admin leaves keywords empty. */
 157    private function keywords(array $data, KnowledgeRetriever $retriever): array
 158    {
 159        $explicit = array_filter(array_map(fn ($k) => Str::lower(trim($k)), explode(',', (string) ($data['keywords_text'] ?? ''))));
 160        $fromTitle = $retriever->tokens($data['title']);
 161
 162        return array_values(array_unique(array_merge($explicit, $fromTitle)));
 163    }
 164}

10 app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PromptTemplateController.php 89 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
   4
   5use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
   6use App\Models\PromptTemplate;
   7use App\Services\TopicClassifier;
   8use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   9
  10class PromptTemplateController extends Controller
  11{
  12    public function index()
  13    {
  14        $templates = PromptTemplate::query()->orderBy('category')->orderBy('language')->get();
  15
  16        return view('admin.templates.index', compact('templates'));
  17    }
  18
  19    public function create()
  20    {
  21        return view('admin.templates.form', ['template' => new PromptTemplate(['category' => 'general', 'language' => 'sw', 'is_active' => true])]);
  22    }
  23
  24    public function store(Request $request)
  25    {
  26        $data = $this->validated($request);
  27        PromptTemplate::query()->create($data);
  28        $this->ensureSingleActive($data);
  29
  30        return redirect()->route('admin.templates.index')->with('success', 'Template created.');
  31    }
  32
  33    public function edit(PromptTemplate $template)
  34    {
  35        return view('admin.templates.form', compact('template'));
  36    }
  37
  38    public function update(Request $request, PromptTemplate $template)
  39    {
  40        $data = $this->validated($request, $template);
  41        $template->update($data);
  42        $this->ensureSingleActive($data, $template->id);
  43
  44        return redirect()->route('admin.templates.index')->with('success', 'Template updated.');
  45    }
  46
  47    public function toggle(PromptTemplate $template)
  48    {
  49        $template->update(['is_active' => ! $template->is_active]);
  50        if ($template->is_active) {
  51            $this->ensureSingleActive($template->only('category', 'language'), $template->id);
  52        }
  53
  54        return back()->with('success', 'Template ' . ($template->is_active ? 'activated' : 'deactivated') . '.');
  55    }
  56
  57    public function destroy(PromptTemplate $template)
  58    {
  59        $template->delete();
  60
  61        return redirect()->route('admin.templates.index')->with('success', 'Template deleted.');
  62    }
  63
  64    private function validated(Request $request, ?PromptTemplate $existing = null): array
  65    {
  66        return $request->validate([
  67            'name' => 'required|string|max:80|unique:prompt_templates,name' . ($existing ? ',' . $existing->id : ''),
  68            'category' => 'required|in:' . implode(',', TopicClassifier::keys()),
  69            'language' => 'required|in:sw,en',
  70            'template' => 'required|string|min:20|max:4000',
  71            'is_active' => 'sometimes|boolean',
  72        ]) + ['is_active' => $request->boolean('is_active')];
  73    }
  74
  75    /** Only one active persona per (category, language). */
  76    private function ensureSingleActive(array $data, ?int $keepId = null): void
  77    {
  78        if (empty($data['is_active']) && $keepId === null) {
  79            return;
  80        }
  81        $keep = $keepId ?? PromptTemplate::query()->where('name', $data['name'])->value('id');
  82        PromptTemplate::query()
  83            ->where('category', $data['category'])
  84            ->where('language', $data['language'])
  85            ->where('id', '!=', $keep)
  86            ->update(['is_active' => false]);
  87        cache()->forget('huru.templates.active');
  88    }
  89}

12 app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SettingController.php 38 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
   4
   5use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
   6use App\Support\Settings;
   7use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   8
   9class SettingController extends Controller
  10{
  11    public function index()
  12    {
  13        return view('admin.settings', [
  14            'groups' => Settings::groups(),
  15            'values' => Settings::all(),
  16        ]);
  17    }
  18
  19    public function update(Request $request)
  20    {
  21        $rules = Settings::rules();
  22
  23        // Unchecked checkboxes are absent from the request: treat as "0".
  24        foreach (Settings::schema() as $key => $def) {
  25            if ($def['type'] === 'boolean' && ! $request->has($key)) {
  26                $request->merge([$key => '0']);
  27            }
  28        }
  29
  30        $data = $request->validate($rules);
  31
  32        foreach ($data as $key => $value) {
  33            Settings::set($key, $value);
  34        }
  35
  36        return back()->with('success', 'Settings saved.');
  37    }
  38}

   1<?php

  50    }

  30    public function show(Request $request, CommunityThread $thread)
  31    {
  32        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
  33        if (! $user) {
  34            return redirect()->route('chat.index', ['redirect' => $request->getRequestUri()]);
  35        }
  36
  37        $isMember = $thread->members()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists();
  38        if ($thread->is_private && ! $isMember) {
  39            return redirect()->route('community.index')->with('error', 'Huu ni mjadala wa faragha. / This is a private thread.');
  40        }
  41
  42        $posts = $thread->posts()->where('is_approved', true)->with('user')->orderBy('created_at')->paginate(50);
  43        $members = $thread->members()->get();
  44
  45        return view('community.show', compact('thread', 'posts', 'members', 'user', 'isMember') + ['settings' => $this->settings()]);
  46    }
  47
  48    public function storeThread(Request $request)
  49    {
  50        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
  51        if (! $user) {
  52            return response()->json(['error' => 'Unauthorized'], 401);
  53        }
  54        if ($user->is_banned) {
  55            return back()->with('error', 'Huduma imefungwa kwa akaunti hii.');
  56        }
  57
  58        $data = $request->validate([
  59            'title' => 'required|string|min:3|max:120',
  60            'description' => 'nullable|string|max:1000',
  61            'is_private' => 'sometimes|boolean',
  62        ]);
  63
  64        $thread = CommunityThread::query()->create([
  65            'title' => $data['title'],
  66            'slug' => Str::slug($data['title']) . '-' . Str::lower(Str::random(5)),
  67            'description' => $data['description'] ?? null,
  68            'creator_id' => $user->id,
  69            'is_private' => $request->boolean('is_private'),
  70        ]);
  71
  72        $thread->members()->attach($user->id, ['role' => 'admin']);
  73
  74        return redirect()->route('community.show', $thread);
  75    }
  76
  77    public function storePost(Request $request, CommunityThread $thread, ModerationService $moderation)
  78    {
  79        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
  80        if (! $user) {
  81            return response()->json(['error' => 'Unauthorized'], 401);
  82        }
  83        if ($user->is_banned) {
  84            return response()->json(['error' => 'Account blocked'], 403);
  85        }
  86        if (! $thread->members()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()) {
  87            return response()->json(['error' => 'Si mwanachama wa mjadala huu. / Not a member of this thread.'], 403);
  88        }
  89
  90        $data = $request->validate(['content' => 'required|string|min:2|max:3000']);
  91
  92        if ($moderation->isAbusive($data['content'])) {
  93            $user->increment('abuse_count');
  94            if ($user->abuse_count >= Settings::int('abuse_strike_limit')) {
  95                $user->forceFill(['is_banned' => true])->save();
  96            }
  97
  98            return response()->json(['error' => 'Lugha ya matusi hairuhusiwi. / Abusive language is not allowed.'], 422);
  99        }
 100
 101        $post = CommunityPost::query()->create([
 102            'community_thread_id' => $thread->id,
 103            'user_id' => $user->id,
 104            'content' => $data['content'],
 105            'is_approved' => ! $thread->is_system, // system threads are moderated before display on the landing page
 106        ]);
 107
 108        if ($request->expectsJson()) {
 109            return response()->json(['status' => 'success', 'post' => $post->load('user'), 'pending' => ! $post->is_approved]);
 110        }
 111
 112        return back()->with('success', $post->is_approved ? 'Ujumbe umetumwa.' : 'Ujumbe umepokelewa na utaonekana baada ya kuhakikiwa.');
 113    }
 114
 115    public function join(Request $request, CommunityThread $thread)
 116    {
 117        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
 118        if (! $user) {
 119            return redirect()->route('chat.index', ['redirect' => route('community.show', $thread, false)]);
 120        }
 121        if ($thread->is_private) {
 122            return back()->with('error', 'Huwezi kujiunga na mjadala wa faragha bila mwaliko.');
 123        }
 124
 125        $thread->members()->syncWithoutDetaching([$user->id => ['role' => 'member']]);
 126
 127        return redirect()->route('community.show', $thread);
 128    }
 129
 130    public function leave(Request $request, CommunityThread $thread)
 131    {
 132        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
 133        if (! $user) {
 134            return redirect()->route('chat.index');
 135        }
 136
 137        $thread->members()->detach($user->id);
 138
 139        return redirect()->route('community.index');
 140    }

 143    {

15 app/Http/Controllers/ContactController.php 70 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4
   5use App\Support\Settings;
   6use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   7use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
   8use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail;
   9use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
  10
  11class ContactController extends Controller
  12{
  13    public function submit(Request $request)
  14    {
  15        $data = $request->validate([
  16            'type' => 'nullable|in:subscribe,partner,general',
  17            'email' => 'required|email|max:120',
  18            'name' => 'nullable|string|max:80',
  19            'organisation' => 'nullable|string|max:120',
  20            'message' => 'nullable|string|max:2000',
  21            'website' => 'nullable|max:0', // honeypot
  22        ]);
  23
  24        $key = 'contact:' . $request->ip();
  25        if (RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($key, 5)) {
  26            return back()->with('contact_error', 'Umetuma mara nyingi. Jaribu tena baadaye.');
  27        }
  28        RateLimiter::hit($key, 3600);
  29
  30        $type = $data['type'] ?? 'general';
  31        $to = Settings::get('contact_email') ?: config('mail.from.address');
  32        $app = Settings::get('app_name');
  33
  34        $subject = match ($type) {
  35            'subscribe' => "[{$app}] New subscriber",
  36            'partner' => "[{$app}] Partnership enquiry",
  37            default => "[{$app}] New enquiry",
  38        };
  39
  40        $body = implode("\n", array_filter([
  41            "Type: {$type}",
  42            'Email: ' . $data['email'],
  43            'Name: ' . ($data['name'] ?? '-'),
  44            'Organisation: ' . ($data['organisation'] ?? '-'),
  45            '',
  46            $data['message'] ?? '',
  47            '',
  48            'Received: ' . now()->toDateTimeString() . ' (' . config('app.timezone') . ')',
  49        ]));
  50
  51        try {
  52            if ($to) {
  53                Mail::raw($body, function ($mail) use ($to, $subject, $data) {
  54                    $mail->to($to)->replyTo($data['email'], $data['name'] ?? 'Visitor')->subject($subject);
  55                });
  56            }
  57            Log::info('Contact form', ['type' => $type, 'email' => $data['email']]);
  58
  59            return back()->with('contact_success', match ($type) {
  60                'partner' => 'Asante! Tutawasiliana nawe ndani ya siku 2 za kazi.',
  61                'subscribe' => 'Umejiunga! Tutakutumia taarifa za maendeleo.',
  62                default => 'Ujumbe wako umepokelewa. Asante.',
  63            });
  64        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
  65            Log::error('Contact mail failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
  66
  67            return back()->with('contact_error', 'Imeshindikana kutuma. Tafadhali tutumie barua pepe moja kwa moja: ' . $to);
  68        }
  69    }
  70}

17 app/Http/Controllers/SmsController.php 68 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4
   5use App\Jobs\ProcessIncomingSms;
   6use App\Support\Phone;
   7use App\Support\Settings;
   8use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   9use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
  10use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
  11
  12class SmsController extends Controller
  13{
  14    /**
  15     * Africa's Talking inbound callback (form-encoded: from, to, text, date, id, linkId).
  16     */
  17    public function inbound(Request $request)
  18    {
  19        $from = Phone::normalize($request->input('from'));
  20        $text = trim((string) $request->input('text', ''));
  21
  22        if (! $from || $text === '') {
  23            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Invalid payload'], 400);
  24        }
  25
  26        // A flood from one number must not become a flood of model calls.
  27        $key = 'sms-inbound:' . $from;
  28        if (RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($key, 20)) {
  29            Log::warning('Inbound SMS rate limited', ['from' => Phone::mask($from)]);
  30
  31            return response()->json(['status' => 'rate_limited']);
  32        }
  33        RateLimiter::hit($key, 60);
  34
  35        $keyword = (string) Settings::get('sms_keyword');
  36        $clean = $text;
  37
  38        // On a shared short code the operator prepends the keyword; strip it when present.
  39        if ($keyword !== '' && preg_match('/^' . preg_quote($keyword, '/') . '\b\s*/iu', $text)) {
  40            $clean = trim(preg_replace('/^' . preg_quote($keyword, '/') . '\b\s*/iu', '', $text));
  41        } elseif ($keyword !== '' && config('services.at.require_keyword')) {
  42            Log::info('SMS ignored: keyword missing', ['from' => Phone::mask($from)]);
  43
  44            return response()->json(['status' => 'ignored']);
  45        }
  46
  47        // A bare keyword is a request for help.
  48        if ($clean === '') {
  49            $clean = 'HELP';
  50        }
  51
  52        Log::info('Inbound SMS accepted', ['from' => Phone::mask($from), 'chars' => strlen($clean)]);
  53
  54        ProcessIncomingSms::dispatch($from, $clean, $request->input('id'));
  55
  56        return response()->json(['status' => 'success']);
  57    }
  58
  59    /**
  60     * Optional delivery report callback from Africa's Talking.
  61     */
  62    public function deliveryReport(Request $request)
  63    {
  64        Log::info('SMS delivery report', $request->only('id', 'status', 'phoneNumber', 'failureReason'));
  65
  66        return response()->json(['status' => 'ok']);
  67    }
  68}

18 app/Http/Controllers/WebChatController.php 333 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4
   5use App\Models\Feedback;
   6use App\Models\Message;
   7use App\Models\User;
   8use App\Services\ConversationService;
   9use App\Services\SmsService;
  10use App\Services\TanzaniaContext;
  11use App\Services\TopicClassifier;
  12use App\Support\Phone;
  13use App\Support\Settings;
  14use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  15use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
  16use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
  17use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
  18use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
  19
  20class WebChatController extends Controller
  21{
  22    private const SESSION_KEY = 'chat_user_id';
  23
  24    public function index()
  25    {
  26        return view('chat', [
  27            'settings' => $this->publicSettings(),
  28        ]);
  29    }
  30
  31    /** Bootstrap data for the page: who is logged in and the public settings. */
  32    public function session(Request $request)
  33    {
  34        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
  35
  36        return response()->json([
  37            'authenticated' => (bool) $user,
  38            'user' => $user ? $this->userPayload($user) : null,
  39            'settings' => $this->publicSettings(),
  40            'csrf' => csrf_token(),
  41        ]);
  42    }
  43
  44    /**
  45     * Step 1 of login. Creates the citizen record if needed and sends an OTP.
  46     * When OTP is disabled (or SMS is not configured in a non-production
  47     * environment) the session is opened directly.
  48     */
  49    public function requestOtp(Request $request, SmsService $sms)
  50    {
  51        $data = $request->validate([
  52            'phone_number' => 'required|string|min:9|max:20',
  53            'language' => 'nullable|in:sw,en',
  54        ]);
  55
  56        $phone = Phone::normalize($data['phone_number']);
  57        if (! $phone) {
  58            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['phone_number' => 'Namba ya simu si sahihi. / Invalid phone number.']);
  59        }
  60
  61        $key = 'otp-request:' . $phone;
  62        if (RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($key, 4)) {
  63            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Umeomba mara nyingi. Subiri dakika 10. / Too many requests. Wait 10 minutes.'], 429);
  64        }
  65        RateLimiter::hit($key, 600);
  66
  67        $user = User::query()->firstOrCreate(['phone_number' => $phone]);
  68        if ($user->is_banned) {
  69            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Huduma imefungwa kwa namba hii. / This number is blocked.'], 403);
  70        }
  71
  72        $language = $data['language'] ?? $user->preferred_language ?? 'sw';
  73        $otpEnabled = Settings::bool('otp_enabled');
  74        $canSend = $sms->isConfigured();
  75
  76        if (! $otpEnabled || (! $canSend && ! app()->environment('production'))) {
  77            $this->openSession($request, $user);
  78
  79            return response()->json([
  80                'status' => 'success',
  81                'otp_required' => false,
  82                'user' => $this->userPayload($user),
  83                'csrf' => csrf_token(), // session was regenerated, token rotated
  84            ]);
  85        }
  86
  87        if (! $canSend) {
  88            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Huduma ya uthibitisho haipatikani kwa sasa. / Verification is unavailable right now.'], 503);
  89        }
  90
  91        $code = (string) random_int(100000, 999999);
  92        $ttl = Settings::int('otp_ttl_minutes');
  93        $user->forceFill([
  94            'otp_hash' => Hash::make($code),
  95            'otp_expires_at' => now()->addMinutes($ttl),
  96            'otp_attempts' => 0,
  97        ])->save();
  98
  99        $sent = $sms->sendOtp($phone, $code, $language, (string) Settings::get('app_name'), $ttl);
 100        if ($sent['status'] === 'error') {
 101            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Imeshindikana kutuma ujumbe wa uthibitisho. / Could not send the verification SMS.'], 502);
 102        }
 103
 104        $payload = ['status' => 'success', 'otp_required' => true, 'phone_masked' => Phone::mask($phone), 'ttl_minutes' => $ttl];
 105        if (config('app.debug') && ! app()->environment('production')) {
 106            $payload['debug_code'] = $code; // never present in production
 107        }
 108
 109        return response()->json($payload);
 110    }
 111
 112    /** Step 2 of login. */
 113    public function verifyOtp(Request $request)
 114    {
 115        $data = $request->validate([
 116            'phone_number' => 'required|string',
 117            'code' => 'required|digits:6',
 118        ]);
 119
 120        $phone = Phone::normalize($data['phone_number']);
 121        $user = $phone ? User::query()->where('phone_number', $phone)->first() : null;
 122
 123        if (! $user || ! $user->otp_hash || ! $user->otp_expires_at || $user->otp_expires_at->isPast()) {
 124            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Namba ya uthibitisho imeisha muda. Omba nyingine. / Code expired, request a new one.'], 422);
 125        }
 126
 127        if ($user->otp_attempts >= 5) {
 128            $user->forceFill(['otp_hash' => null, 'otp_expires_at' => null])->save();
 129
 130            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Majaribio mengi. Omba namba mpya. / Too many attempts, request a new code.'], 429);
 131        }
 132
 133        if (! Hash::check($data['code'], $user->otp_hash)) {
 134            $user->increment('otp_attempts');
 135
 136            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Namba ya uthibitisho si sahihi. / Incorrect code.'], 422);
 137        }
 138
 139        $user->forceFill(['otp_hash' => null, 'otp_expires_at' => null, 'otp_attempts' => 0])->save();
 140        $this->openSession($request, $user);
 141
 142        return response()->json(['status' => 'success', 'user' => $this->userPayload($user), 'csrf' => csrf_token()]);
 143    }
 144
 145    public function messages(Request $request)
 146    {
 147        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
 148        if (! $user) {
 149            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Unauthorized'], 401);
 150        }
 151
 152        $query = Message::query()->where('user_id', $user->id)->with('feedback');
 153
 154        $filtering = false;
 155        if ($request->filled('keyword')) {
 156            $query->where('content', 'like', '%' . str_replace(['%', '_'], ['\%', '\_'], $request->string('keyword')) . '%');
 157            $filtering = true;
 158        }
 159        if ($request->filled('date')) {
 160            $query->whereDate('created_at', $request->string('date'));
 161            $filtering = true;
 162        }
 163        if ($request->filled('category')) {
 164            $query->where('category', $request->string('category'));
 165            $filtering = true;
 166        }
 167        if ($request->filled('before')) {
 168            $query->where('id', '<', (int) $request->input('before'));
 169        }
 170
 171        $limit = $filtering ? 200 : Settings::int('web_chat_limit');
 172        $messages = $query->latest('id')->take($limit)->get()->reverse()->values();
 173
 174        return response()->json([
 175            'status' => 'success',
 176            'messages' => $messages->map(fn (Message $m) => $this->messagePayload($m)),
 177            'has_more' => $messages->isNotEmpty() && Message::query()->where('user_id', $user->id)->where('id', '<', $messages->first()->id)->exists(),
 178        ]);
 179    }
 180
 181    public function send(Request $request, ConversationService $conversation)
 182    {
 183        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
 184        if (! $user) {
 185            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Unauthorized'], 401);
 186        }
 187
 188        $data = $request->validate(['message' => 'required|string|max:2000']);
 189
 190        $result = $conversation->handle($user, $data['message'], Message::CHANNEL_WEB);
 191
 192        if ($result['kind'] === ConversationService::KIND_IGNORED) {
 193            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Empty message'], 400);
 194        }
 195
 196        return response()->json([
 197            'status' => 'success',
 198            'kind' => $result['kind'],
 199            'category' => $result['category'],
 200            'category_label' => $result['category'] ? TopicClassifier::label($result['category'], $result['language']) : null,
 201            'user_message' => $result['inbound'] ? $this->messagePayload($result['inbound']) : null,
 202            'reply' => $result['outbound']
 203                ? $this->messagePayload($result['outbound'])
 204                : ['id' => null, 'direction' => 'outbound', 'content' => $result['text'], 'created_at' => now()->toIso8601String(), 'category' => $result['category'], 'feedback' => null],
 205        ]);
 206    }

 208    public function feedback(Request $request)
 209    {
 210        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
 211        if (! $user) {
 212            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Unauthorized'], 401);
 213        }
 214
 215        $data = $request->validate([
 216            'message_id' => 'required|integer',
 217            'rating' => 'required|in:1,-1',
 218            'comment' => 'nullable|string|max:500',
 219        ]);
 220
 221        $message = Message::query()->where('user_id', $user->id)->where('direction', Message::DIRECTION_OUT)->find($data['message_id']);
 222        if (! $message) {
 223            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Message not found'], 404);
 224        }
 225
 226        Feedback::query()->updateOrCreate(
 227            ['message_id' => $message->id, 'user_id' => $user->id],
 228            ['rating' => (int) $data['rating'], 'comment' => $data['comment'] ?? null]
 229        );
 230
 231        return response()->json(['status' => 'success']);
 232    }
 233
 234    public function preferences(Request $request, TanzaniaContext $tz)
 235    {
 236        $user = $this->currentUser($request);
 237        if (! $user) {
 238            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Unauthorized'], 401);
 239        }
 240
 241        $data = $request->validate([
 242            'name' => 'nullable|string|max:40',
 243            'preferred_language' => 'nullable|in:sw,en,auto',
 244            'region' => 'nullable|string|max:40',
 245        ]);
 246
 247        $update = [];
 248        if (array_key_exists('name', $data)) {
 249            $update['name'] = $data['name'] ? trim($data['name']) : null;
 250        }
 251        if (array_key_exists('preferred_language', $data)) {
 252            $update['preferred_language'] = $data['preferred_language'] === 'auto' ? null : $data['preferred_language'];
 253        }
 254        if (array_key_exists('region', $data)) {
 255            $loc = $data['region'] ? $tz->detectLocation($data['region']) : null;
 256            $update['region'] = $loc['region'] ?? null;
 257        }
 258
 259        $user->forceFill($update)->save();
 260
 261        return response()->json(['status' => 'success', 'user' => $this->userPayload($user->fresh())]);
 262    }
 263
 264    public function logout(Request $request)
 265    {
 266        $request->session()->forget(self::SESSION_KEY);
 267        $request->session()->regenerate();
 268
 269        return response()->json(['status' => 'success', 'csrf' => csrf_token()]);
 270    }
 271
 272    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
 273
 274    private function openSession(Request $request, User $user): void
 275    {
 276        $request->session()->regenerate();
 277        $request->session()->put(self::SESSION_KEY, $user->id);
 278        $user->forceFill(['last_seen_at' => now()])->saveQuietly();
 279    }
 280
 281    private function currentUser(Request $request): ?User
 282    {
 283        $id = $request->session()->get(self::SESSION_KEY);
 284        if (! $id) {
 285            return null;
 286        }
 287        $user = User::query()->find($id);
 288        if (! $user) {
 289            $request->session()->forget(self::SESSION_KEY);
 290        }
 291
 292        return $user;
 293    }
 294
 295    private function userPayload(User $user): array
 296    {
 297        return [
 298            'id' => $user->id,
 299            'name' => $user->name,
 300            'display_name' => $user->displayName(),
 301            'phone_masked' => Phone::mask($user->phone_number),
 302            'preferred_language' => $user->preferred_language ?? 'auto',
 303            'region' => $user->region ? ucwords($user->region) : null,
 304            'is_banned' => $user->is_banned,
 305        ];
 306    }
 307
 308    private function messagePayload(Message $m): array
 309    {
 310        return [
 311            'id' => $m->id,
 312            'direction' => $m->direction,
 313            'content' => $m->content,
 314            'category' => $m->category,
 315            'category_label' => $m->category ? TopicClassifier::label($m->category, $m->language ?? 'sw') : null,
 316            'language' => $m->language,
 317            'created_at' => $m->created_at?->toIso8601String(),
 318            'feedback' => $m->relationLoaded('feedback') && $m->feedback ? $m->feedback->rating : null,
 319        ];
 320    }
 321
 322    private function publicSettings(): array
 323    {
 324        return [
 325            'app_name' => Settings::get('app_name'),
 326            'sms_keyword' => Settings::get('sms_keyword'),
 327            'sms_shortcode' => Settings::get('sms_shortcode'),
 328            'otp_enabled' => Settings::bool('otp_enabled'),
 329            'categories' => collect(TopicClassifier::CATEGORIES)->map(fn ($l, $k) => ['key' => $k, 'sw' => $l['sw'], 'en' => $l['en']])->values(),
 330            'regions' => array_map('ucwords', TanzaniaContext::REGIONS),
 331        ];
 332    }
 333}

19 app/Http/Middleware/EnsureAdmin.php 25 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Middleware;
   4
   5use Closure;
   6use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   7use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;
   8
   9class EnsureAdmin
  10{
  11    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
  12    {
  13        $user = $request->user();
  14
  15        if (! $user) {
  16            return redirect()->route('admin.login')->with('error', 'Please sign in.');
  17        }
  18
  19        if (! $user->isAdmin()) {
  20            abort(403, 'Admin access only.');
  21        }
  22
  23        return $next($request);
  24    }
  25}

20 app/Http/Middleware/VerifySmsWebhook.php 43 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Http\Middleware;
   4
   5use Closure;
   6use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   7use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
   8use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;
   9
  10/**
  11 * Protects the inbound SMS callback. Africa's Talking does not sign
  12 * callbacks, so the callback URL registered in their dashboard must carry
  13 * a shared secret: https://your-host/api/sms/inbound?token=<AT_WEBHOOK_SECRET>
  14 * (a X-Webhook-Token header works too).
  15 */
  16class VerifySmsWebhook
  17{
  18    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
  19    {
  20        $expected = (string) config('services.at.webhook_secret');
  21
  22        if ($expected === '') {
  23            if (app()->environment('production')) {
  24                Log::critical('AT_WEBHOOK_SECRET is not set; refusing inbound SMS in production.');
  25
  26                return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'webhook secret not configured'], 503);
  27            }
  28            Log::warning('AT_WEBHOOK_SECRET is not set; accepting inbound SMS because APP_ENV is not production.');
  29
  30            return $next($request);
  31        }
  32
  33        $given = (string) ($request->query('token') ?? $request->header('X-Webhook-Token') ?? '');
  34
  35        if ($given === '' || ! hash_equals($expected, $given)) {
  36            Log::warning('Rejected inbound SMS callback with bad token', ['ip' => $request->ip()]);
  37
  38            return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'unauthorized'], 401);
  39        }
  40
  41        return $next($request);
  42    }
  43}

21 app/Jobs/ProcessIncomingSms.php 57 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Jobs;
   4
   5use App\Models\Message;
   6use App\Models\User;
   7use App\Services\ConversationService;
   8use App\Services\SmsService;
   9use App\Support\Phone;
  10use Illuminate\Contracts\Queue\ShouldBeUnique;
  11use Illuminate\Contracts\Queue\ShouldQueue;
  12use Illuminate\Foundation\Queue\Queueable;
  13use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
  14
  15class ProcessIncomingSms implements ShouldQueue, ShouldBeUnique
  16{
  17    use Queueable;
  18
  19    public int $tries = 2;
  20
  21    public int $timeout = 90;
  22
  23    public function __construct(
  24        public readonly string $from,
  25        public readonly string $text,
  26        public readonly ?string $providerMessageId = null,
  27    ) {
  28    }
  29
  30    public function handle(ConversationService $conversation, SmsService $sms): void
  31    {
  32        $user = User::query()->firstOrCreate(['phone_number' => $this->from]);
  33
  34        $result = $conversation->handle($user, $this->text, Message::CHANNEL_SMS);
  35
  36        if (empty($result['text'])) {
  37            Log::info('No SMS reply', ['from' => Phone::mask($this->from), 'kind' => $result['kind']]);
  38
  39            return;
  40        }
  41
  42        $send = $sms->send($this->from, $result['text']);
  43
  44        Log::info('SMS reply', [
  45            'from' => Phone::mask($this->from),
  46            'kind' => $result['kind'],
  47            'category' => $result['category'],
  48            'delivery' => $send['status'],
  49        ]);
  50    }
  51
  52    /** Use the job's own key so retries do not duplicate replies. */
  53    public function uniqueId(): string
  54    {
  55        return $this->providerMessageId ?: md5($this->from . '|' . $this->text);
  56    }
  57}

35 app/Models/User.php 100 lines

   1<?php

   9class User extends Authenticatable
  10{

  13    public const ROLE_USER = 'user';
  14    public const ROLE_ADMIN = 'admin';
  15
  16    protected $fillable = [
  17        'name',

  20        'phone_number',

  23        'region',
  24        'is_banned',
  25        'abuse_count',
  26        'opted_out',
  27        'otp_hash',
  28        'otp_expires_at',
  29        'otp_attempts',
  30        'last_seen_at',
  31    ];

  37    ];

  42            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
  43            'password' => 'hashed',
  44            'is_banned' => 'boolean',

  46            'abuse_count' => 'integer',
  47            'otp_attempts' => 'integer',

  49            'last_seen_at' => 'datetime',
  50        ];
  51    }

  76            ->withPivot('role', 'joined_at')
  77            ->withTimestamps();

  81    {
  82        return $this->hasMany(CommunityThread::class, 'creator_id');
  83    }

  86    {
  87        return $this->hasMany(CommunityPost::class);
  88    }
  89
  90    /** Display name that never leaks the full phone number to other citizens. */
  91    public function displayName(): string
  92    {
  93        if ($this->name) {
  94            return $this->name;
  95        }
  96        $digits = preg_replace('/\D/', '', (string) $this->phone_number);
  97
  98        return 'Mwananchi ' . substr($digits, -4);

36 app/Providers/AppServiceProvider.php 37 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Providers;
   4
   5use App\Services\Ai\GeminiClient;
   6use App\Services\Ai\LlmClient;
   7use Composer\CaBundle\CaBundle;
   8use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
   9use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Vite;
  10use Illuminate\Support\ServiceProvider;
  11
  12class AppServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider
  13{
  14    public function register(): void
  15    {
  16        $this->app->singleton(LlmClient::class, function () {
  17            return new GeminiClient(
  18                apiKey: config('services.gemini.key'),
  19                baseUrl: config('services.gemini.url'),
  20                timeoutSeconds: (int) config('services.gemini.timeout', 30),
  21            );
  22        });
  23    }
  24
  25    public function boot(): void
  26    {
  27        Vite::useBuildDirectory('build');
  28
  29        // Keep TLS verification ON everywhere, even on machines (typically
  30        // Windows dev boxes) whose PHP has no CA bundle configured.
  31        // composer/ca-bundle prefers the system bundle and falls back to
  32        // its own up-to-date cacert.pem.
  33        Http::globalOptions([
  34            'verify' => CaBundle::getSystemCaRootBundlePath(),
  35        ]);
  36    }
  37}

37 app/Services/Ai/GeminiClient.php 131 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services\Ai;
   4
   5use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
   6use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
   7
   8/**
   9 * Google Gemini generateContent client.
  10 *
  11 * - Sends the API key in a header, never in the URL (keeps it out of logs).
  12 * - Keeps TLS verification on.
  13 * - Uses a real system instruction and multi-turn contents.
  14 * - Normalises the response into a provider-agnostic shape.
  15 */
  16class GeminiClient implements LlmClient
  17{
  18    public function __construct(
  19        private readonly ?string $apiKey,
  20        private readonly string $baseUrl = 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models',
  21        private readonly int $timeoutSeconds = 30,
  22    ) {
  23    }
  24
  25    public function isConfigured(): bool
  26    {
  27        return ! empty($this->apiKey);
  28    }
  29
  30    public function generate(string $systemInstruction, array $turns, array $options = []): array
  31    {
  32        $model = $options['model'] ?? 'gemini-flash-lite-latest';
  33        $base = [
  34            'status' => self::STATUS_ERROR,
  35            'text' => null,
  36            'model' => $model,
  37            'tokens' => ['prompt' => 0, 'completion' => 0, 'total' => 0],
  38            'latency_ms' => 0,
  39            'finish_reason' => null,
  40            'error' => null,
  41        ];
  42
  43        if (! $this->isConfigured()) {
  44            Log::critical('Gemini API key missing.');
  45
  46            return array_merge($base, ['status' => self::STATUS_UNCONFIGURED, 'error' => 'missing_api_key']);
  47        }
  48
  49        $contents = [];
  50        foreach ($turns as $turn) {
  51            $contents[] = [
  52                'role' => $turn['role'] === 'model' ? 'model' : 'user',
  53                'parts' => [['text' => (string) $turn['text']]],
  54            ];
  55        }
  56
  57        $payload = [
  58            'systemInstruction' => ['parts' => [['text' => $systemInstruction]]],
  59            'contents' => $contents,
  60            'generationConfig' => [
  61                'temperature' => (float) ($options['temperature'] ?? 0.4),
  62                'maxOutputTokens' => (int) ($options['max_tokens'] ?? 2048),
  63            ],
  64            'safetySettings' => [
  65                ['category' => 'HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT', 'threshold' => 'BLOCK_ONLY_HIGH'],
  66                ['category' => 'HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH', 'threshold' => 'BLOCK_ONLY_HIGH'],
  67                ['category' => 'HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT', 'threshold' => 'BLOCK_MEDIUM_AND_ABOVE'],
  68                ['category' => 'HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT', 'threshold' => 'BLOCK_ONLY_HIGH'],
  69            ],
  70        ];
  71
  72        $started = microtime(true);
  73
  74        try {
  75            $response = Http::timeout($this->timeoutSeconds)
  76                ->withHeaders([
  77                    'Content-Type' => 'application/json',
  78                    'x-goog-api-key' => $this->apiKey,
  79                ])
  80                ->retry(2, 800, throw: false)
  81                ->post(rtrim($this->baseUrl, '/') . '/' . rawurlencode($model) . ':generateContent', $payload);
  82
  83            $latency = (int) round((microtime(true) - $started) * 1000);
  84            $base['latency_ms'] = $latency;
  85
  86            if (! $response->successful()) {
  87                $body = $response->json();
  88                $message = $body['error']['message'] ?? ('HTTP ' . $response->status());
  89                Log::error('Gemini request failed', ['status' => $response->status(), 'message' => $message]);
  90
  91                return array_merge($base, ['error' => $message]);
  92            }
  93
  94            $data = $response->json();
  95            $usage = $data['usageMetadata'] ?? [];
  96            $base['tokens'] = [
  97                'prompt' => (int) ($usage['promptTokenCount'] ?? 0),
  98                'completion' => (int) ($usage['candidatesTokenCount'] ?? 0),
  99                'total' => (int) ($usage['totalTokenCount'] ?? 0),
 100            ];
 101
 102            $candidate = $data['candidates'][0] ?? null;
 103            $finish = $candidate['finishReason'] ?? null;
 104            $base['finish_reason'] = $finish;
 105
 106            $blockReason = $data['promptFeedback']['blockReason'] ?? null;
 107            if ($blockReason || $finish === 'SAFETY' || $finish === 'PROHIBITED_CONTENT') {
 108                return array_merge($base, ['status' => self::STATUS_SAFETY]);
 109            }
 110
 111            $text = '';
 112            foreach ($candidate['content']['parts'] ?? [] as $part) {
 113                $text .= $part['text'] ?? '';
 114            }
 115            $text = trim($text);
 116
 117            if ($text === '') {
 118                return array_merge($base, ['error' => 'empty_response']);
 119            }
 120
 121            return array_merge($base, ['status' => self::STATUS_OK, 'text' => $text]);
 122        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
 123            Log::error('Gemini client exception: ' . $e->getMessage());
 124
 125            return array_merge($base, [
 126                'latency_ms' => (int) round((microtime(true) - $started) * 1000),
 127                'error' => $e->getMessage(),
 128            ]);
 129        }
 130    }
 131}
