39 app/Services/AiService.php 59 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services;
   4
   5use App\Services\Ai\LlmClient;
   6use App\Support\Settings;
   7
   8/**
   9 * Thin orchestration between the prompt engine and the model client.
  10 */
  11class AiService
  12{
  13    public function __construct(
  14        private readonly PromptEngine $engine,
  15        private readonly LlmClient $client,
  16    ) {
  17    }
  18
  19    /**
  20     * @param  array  $ctx  See PromptEngine::build()
  21     * @return array{status:string,text:?string,model:string,tokens:array,latency_ms:int,error:?string,system:string,knowledge:\Illuminate\Support\Collection}
  22     */
  23    public function answer(array $ctx): array
  24    {
  25        $built = $this->engine->build($ctx);
  26
  27        $result = $this->client->generate($built['system'], $built['turns'], [
  28            'model' => Settings::get('ai_model'),
  29            'temperature' => Settings::float('ai_temperature'),
  30            'max_tokens' => Settings::int('ai_max_tokens'),
  31        ]);
  32
  33        if ($result['status'] === LlmClient::STATUS_OK) {
  34            $result['text'] = $this->clean($result['text']);
  35        }
  36
  37        $result['system'] = $built['system'];
  38        $result['knowledge'] = $built['knowledge'];
  39
  40        return $result;
  41    }
  42
  43    /** Strip markdown the model may still emit, since SMS cannot render it. */
  44    private function clean(string $text): string
  45    {
  46        $text = preg_replace('/\*\*?/', '', $text);
  47        $text = preg_replace('/^#{1,6}\s*/m', '', $text);
  48        $text = preg_replace('/`+/', '', $text);
  49        $text = preg_replace('/^\s*[-•]\s+/m', '- ', $text);
  50        $text = preg_replace('/\n{3,}/', "\n\n", $text);
  51
  52        return trim($text);
  53    }
  54
  55    public function isConfigured(): bool
  56    {
  57        return $this->client->isConfigured();
  58    }
  59}

40 app/Services/ConversationService.php 341 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services;
   4
   5use App\Models\AiLog;
   6use App\Models\Message;
   7use App\Models\User;
   8use App\Services\Ai\LlmClient;
   9use App\Support\Settings;
  10use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
  11use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
  12use Illuminate\Support\Str;
  13
  14/**
  15 * One entry point for every channel. Takes a citizen and their text,
  16 * returns what to send back. The SMS job and the web controller both call
  17 * this, so behaviour (commands, moderation, memory, logging) never drifts
  18 * between channels.
  19 */
  20class ConversationService
  21{
  22    public const KIND_ANSWER = 'answer';
  23    public const KIND_COMMAND = 'command';
  24    public const KIND_MODERATION = 'moderation';
  25    public const KIND_MAINTENANCE = 'maintenance';
  26    public const KIND_BANNED = 'banned';
  27    public const KIND_IGNORED = 'ignored';
  28    public const KIND_RATE_LIMITED = 'rate_limited';
  29    public const KIND_ERROR = 'error';
  30
  31    public function __construct(
  32        private readonly AiService $ai,
  33        private readonly ModerationService $moderation,
  34        private readonly LanguageDetector $detector,
  35        private readonly TopicClassifier $classifier,
  36        private readonly TanzaniaContext $tz,
  37    ) {
  38    }
  39
  40    /**
  41     * @return array{kind:string, text:?string, inbound:?Message, outbound:?Message, category:?string, language:string}
  42     */
  43    public function handle(User $user, string $text, string $channel): array
  44    {
  45        $text = trim($text);
  46        $user->forceFill(['last_seen_at' => now()])->saveQuietly();
  47
  48        $language = $this->resolveLanguage($user, $text);
  49        $result = ['kind' => self::KIND_IGNORED, 'text' => null, 'inbound' => null, 'outbound' => null, 'category' => null, 'language' => $language];
  50
  51        if ($text === '') {
  52            return $result;
  53        }
  54
  55        // 1. Commands (HELP, STOP, LUGHA, JINA, MKOA ...) never hit the model.
  56        if ($command = $this->handleCommand($user, $text, $language, $channel)) {
  57            $inbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_IN, $channel, $text, $language, null);
  58            $outbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_OUT, $channel, $command, $language, null);
  59
  60            return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_COMMAND, 'text' => $command, 'inbound' => $inbound, 'outbound' => $outbound]);
  61        }
  62
  63        // 2. Opted-out SMS users are left alone until they send START/ANZA.
  64        if ($user->opted_out && $channel === Message::CHANNEL_SMS) {
  65            return $result;
  66        }
  67
  68        // 3. Banned users get one short notice, nothing else.
  69        if ($user->is_banned) {
  70            $msg = $this->moderation->getBanMessage($language);
  71
  72            return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_BANNED, 'text' => $msg]);
  73        }
  74
  75        // 4. Per-user rate limit.
  76        $limitKey = 'huru:ask:' . $user->id;
  77        if (RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($limitKey, Settings::int('rate_limit_per_minute'))) {
  78            $msg = $language === 'en'
  79                ? 'You are sending questions too quickly. Please wait a minute and try again.'
  80                : 'Umetuma maswali mengi kwa haraka. Tafadhali subiri dakika moja kisha ujaribu tena.';
  81
  82            return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_RATE_LIMITED, 'text' => $msg]);
  83        }
  84        RateLimiter::hit($limitKey, 60);
  85
  86        $classification = $this->classifier->classify($text);
  87        $category = $classification['category'];
  88        $result['category'] = $category;
  89
  90        $inbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_IN, $channel, $text, $language, $category);
  91        $result['inbound'] = $inbound;
  92
  93        // 5. Word-list moderation with strikes.
  94        if ($this->moderation->isAbusive($text)) {
  95            $msg = $this->strike($user, $language);
  96            $outbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_OUT, $channel, $msg, $language, $category);
  97
  98            return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_MODERATION, 'text' => $msg, 'outbound' => $outbound]);
  99        }
 100
 101        // 6. Maintenance switch.
 102        if (! Settings::bool('ai_enabled')) {
 103            $msg = (string) Settings::get($language === 'en' ? 'ai_maintenance_message_en' : 'ai_maintenance_message_sw');
 104            $outbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_OUT, $channel, $msg, $language, $category);
 105
 106            return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_MAINTENANCE, 'text' => $msg, 'outbound' => $outbound]);
 107        }
 108
 109        // 7. Ask the engine with full context.
 110        $location = $this->tz->detectLocation($text);
 111        if ($location && ! $user->region) {
 112            $user->forceFill(['region' => $location['region']])->saveQuietly();
 113        }
 114
 115        $answer = $this->ai->answer([
 116            'question' => $text,
 117            'language' => $language,
 118            'category' => $category,
 119            'channel' => $channel,
 120            'is_emergency' => $classification['is_emergency'],
 121            'location' => $location,
 122            'history' => $this->history($user, $inbound->id),
 123            'user_name' => $user->name,
 124            'user_region' => $user->region,
 125        ]);
 126
 127        if ($answer['status'] === LlmClient::STATUS_SAFETY) {
 128            $msg = $this->strike($user, $language);
 129            $outbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_OUT, $channel, $msg, $language, $category);
 130            $this->log($outbound, $answer, $category, $text);
 131
 132            return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_MODERATION, 'text' => $msg, 'outbound' => $outbound]);
 133        }
 134
 135        if ($answer['status'] !== LlmClient::STATUS_OK) {
 136            $msg = $language === 'en'
 137                ? 'Sorry, the service could not answer right now. Please try again shortly.'
 138                : 'Samahani, huduma haikuweza kujibu kwa sasa. Tafadhali jaribu tena baada ya muda mfupi.';
 139            $outbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_OUT, $channel, $msg, $language, $category);
 140            $this->log($outbound, $answer, $category, $text);
 141            Log::warning('Engine failure', ['user' => $user->id, 'status' => $answer['status'], 'error' => $answer['error']]);
 142
 143            return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_ERROR, 'text' => $msg, 'outbound' => $outbound]);
 144        }
 145
 146        $outbound = $this->store($user, Message::DIRECTION_OUT, $channel, $answer['text'], $language, $category);
 147        $this->log($outbound, $answer, $category, $text);
 148
 149        return array_merge($result, ['kind' => self::KIND_ANSWER, 'text' => $answer['text'], 'outbound' => $outbound]);
 150    }
 151
 152    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
 153
 154    public function resolveLanguage(User $user, string $text): string
 155    {
 156        if (in_array($user->preferred_language, ['sw', 'en'], true)) {
 157            // A stored preference wins unless the citizen clearly wrote the other language.
 158            $detected = $this->detector->detect($text);
 159
 160            return $detected ?: $user->preferred_language;
 161        }
 162
 163        $policy = (string) Settings::get('primary_language');
 164        if ($policy === 'sw' || $policy === 'en') {
 165            return $policy;
 166        }
 167
 168        return $this->detector->detect($text) ?: 'sw';
 169    }
 170
 171    /** Returns the reply for a service command, or null if the text is not a command. */
 172    private function handleCommand(User $user, string $text, string $language, string $channel): ?string
 173    {
 174        $upper = Str::upper(trim($text));
 175        $parts = preg_split('/\s+/', $upper, 2);
 176        $verb = $parts[0] ?? '';
 177        $arg = trim($parts[1] ?? '');
 178        $app = Settings::get('app_name');
 179
 180        if (in_array($verb, ['HELP', 'MSAADA', 'MENU', '?'], true) && $arg === '') {
 181            return $this->helpText($language);
 182        }
 183
 184        if (in_array($verb, ['STOP', 'ACHA', 'SITAKI', 'ONDOA'], true) && $arg === '') {
 185            $user->forceFill(['opted_out' => true])->saveQuietly();
 186
 187            return $language === 'en'
 188                ? "You will no longer receive replies from {$app}. Send ANZA or START any time to resume."
 189                : "Hutapokea tena majibu kutoka {$app}. Tuma ANZA au START wakati wowote kuendelea.";
 190        }
 191
 192        if (in_array($verb, ['START', 'ANZA', 'RUDI'], true) && $arg === '') {
 193            $user->forceFill(['opted_out' => false])->saveQuietly();
 194
 195            return $language === 'en'
 196                ? "Welcome back to {$app}. Send any question to begin."
 197                : "Karibu tena {$app}. Tuma swali lolote kuanza.";
 198        }
 199
 200        if (in_array($verb, ['LUGHA', 'LANG', 'LANGUAGE'], true)) {
 201            $choice = match (true) {
 202                in_array($arg, ['SW', 'KISWAHILI', 'SWAHILI'], true) => 'sw',
 203                in_array($arg, ['EN', 'ENGLISH', 'KIINGEREZA'], true) => 'en',
 204                $arg === '' || in_array($arg, ['AUTO', 'YOYOTE'], true) => null,
 205                default => false,
 206            };
 207            if ($choice === false) {
 208                return $language === 'en'
 209                    ? 'Send LUGHA SW for Swahili, LUGHA EN for English, or LUGHA AUTO to detect automatically.'
 210                    : 'Tuma LUGHA SW kwa Kiswahili, LUGHA EN kwa Kiingereza, au LUGHA AUTO kutambua yenyewe.';
 211            }
 212            $user->forceFill(['preferred_language' => $choice])->saveQuietly();
 213
 214            return match ($choice) {
 215                'sw' => 'Sawa. Kuanzia sasa nitakujibu kwa Kiswahili.',
 216                'en' => 'Done. From now on I will reply in English.',
 217                default => $language === 'en' ? 'Done. I will follow the language you write in.' : 'Sawa. Nitafuata lugha unayoandika.',
 218            };
 219        }
 220
 221        if (in_array($verb, ['JINA', 'NAME'], true) && $arg !== '' && strlen($arg) <= 40) {
 222            $name = Str::title(Str::lower($arg));
 223            $user->forceFill(['name' => $name])->saveQuietly();
 224
 225            return $language === 'en' ? "Thank you, {$name}. I have saved your name." : "Asante, {$name}. Nimehifadhi jina lako.";
 226        }
 227
 228        if (in_array($verb, ['MKOA', 'REGION'], true) && $arg !== '') {
 229            $loc = $this->tz->detectLocation($arg);
 230            if (! $loc) {
 231                return $language === 'en'
 232                    ? 'I could not recognise that region. Example: MKOA Mwanza'
 233                    : 'Sikutambua mkoa huo. Mfano: MKOA Mwanza';
 234            }
 235            $user->forceFill(['region' => $loc['region']])->saveQuietly();
 236            $label = Str::title($loc['region']);
 237
 238            return $language === 'en'
 239                ? "Saved. I will prioritise services in {$label} when you ask for help nearby."
 240                : "Nimehifadhi. Nitatoa kipaumbele kwa huduma za {$label} utakapoomba msaada wa karibu.";
 241        }
 242
 243        return null;
 244    }
 245
 246    public function helpText(string $language): string
 247    {
 248        $app = Settings::get('app_name');
 249        $kw = Settings::get('sms_keyword');
 250        $code = Settings::get('sms_shortcode');
 251
 252        if ($language === 'en') {
 253            return "{$app}: ask anything about life in Tanzania - law, health, farming, school subjects, government services, money, jobs. "
 254                . "SMS: send {$kw} followed by your question to {$code}. "
 255                . 'Commands: LUGHA SW/EN (language), JINA <name>, MKOA <region>, STOP to pause, START to resume.';
 256        }
 257
 258        return "{$app}: uliza lolote kuhusu maisha Tanzania - sheria, afya, kilimo, masomo, huduma za serikali, fedha, ajira. "
 259            . "SMS: tuma {$kw} kisha swali lako kwenda {$code}. "
 260            . 'Amri: LUGHA SW/EN (lugha), JINA <jina lako>, MKOA <mkoa wako>, ACHA kusitisha, ANZA kuendelea.';
 261    }
 262
 263    private function strike(User $user, string $language): string
 264    {
 265        $limit = Settings::int('abuse_strike_limit');
 266        $user->increment('abuse_count');
 267        $user->refresh();
 268
 269        if ($user->abuse_count >= $limit) {
 270            $user->forceFill(['is_banned' => true])->saveQuietly();
 271            Log::warning('User banned for abuse', ['user' => $user->id]);
 272
 273            return $this->moderation->getBanMessage($language);
 274        }
 275
 276        return $this->moderation->getWarningMessage($language, $user->abuse_count, $limit);
 277    }
 278
 279    /** Previous question/answer pairs for continuity, oldest first. */
 280    private function history(User $user, int $excludeMessageId): array
 281    {
 282        $turns = Settings::int('history_turns');
 283        if ($turns <= 0) {
 284            return [];
 285        }
 286
 287        $recent = Message::query()
 288            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
 289            ->where('id', '<', $excludeMessageId)
 290            ->where('created_at', '>=', now()->subDays(2))
 291            ->whereNotNull('category')
 292            ->latest('id')
 293            ->take($turns * 2)
 294            ->get()
 295            ->reverse()
 296            ->values();
 297
 298        $history = [];
 299        foreach ($recent as $m) {
 300            $history[] = [
 301                'role' => $m->direction === Message::DIRECTION_IN ? 'user' : 'model',
 302                'text' => Str::limit($m->content, 600, ''),
 303            ];
 304        }
 305
 306        // Ensure we start with a user turn so the model sees valid alternation.
 307        while (! empty($history) && $history[0]['role'] !== 'user') {
 308            array_shift($history);
 309        }
 310
 311        return $history;
 312    }
 313
 314    private function store(User $user, string $direction, string $channel, string $content, string $language, ?string $category): Message
 315    {
 316        return Message::create([
 317            'user_id' => $user->id,
 318            'direction' => $direction,
 319            'channel' => $channel,
 320            'content' => $content,
 321            'language' => $language,
 322            'category' => $category,
 323        ]);
 324    }
 325
 326    private function log(Message $outbound, array $answer, ?string $category, string $question): void
 327    {
 328        AiLog::create([
 329            'message_id' => $outbound->id,
 330            'model' => $answer['model'] ?? 'unknown',
 331            'category' => $category,
 332            'status' => $answer['status'] ?? 'error',
 333            'prompt' => $question . "\n\n--- SYSTEM ---\n" . Str::limit($answer['system'] ?? '', 12000, '...'),
 334            'response' => (string) ($answer['text'] ?? $answer['error'] ?? ''),
 335            'prompt_tokens' => $answer['tokens']['prompt'] ?? 0,
 336            'completion_tokens' => $answer['tokens']['completion'] ?? 0,
 337            'total_tokens' => $answer['tokens']['total'] ?? 0,
 338            'latency_ms' => $answer['latency_ms'] ?? 0,
 339        ]);
 340    }
 341}

41 app/Services/KnowledgeRetriever.php 157 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services;
   4
   5use App\Models\KnowledgeEntry;
   6use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
   7use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;
   8use Illuminate\Support\Str;
   9
  10/**
  11 * Scores curated knowledge entries against a question.
  12 *
  13 * Tokens are normalised and lightly stemmed for both Swahili and English
  14 * so that "nimekamatwa", "kukamatwa" and "kamatwa" all meet the keyword
  15 * "kamat". Entries in the citizen's language are preferred; other-language
  16 * entries are still searched at a discount so a Swahili question can draw
  17 * on an English-only fact block when nothing else matches.
  18 */
  19class KnowledgeRetriever
  20{
  21    private const STOP = [
  22        // Swahili
  23        'na', 'ya', 'wa', 'za', 'la', 'cha', 'vya', 'kwa', 'ni', 'si', 'je', 'nini', 'vipi', 'gani', 'wapi', 'lini', 'nani',
  24        'mbona', 'kama', 'jinsi', 'hii', 'hiyo', 'hili', 'hiki', 'yangu', 'wangu', 'langu', 'mimi', 'wewe', 'yeye', 'sisi',
  25        'wao', 'sana', 'tu', 'lakini', 'au', 'pia', 'kuhusu', 'katika', 'kutoka', 'hadi', 'sasa', 'leo', 'naomba', 'nataka',
  26        'nahitaji', 'tafadhali', 'asante', 'habari', 'huru', 'msaada', 'nieleze', 'niambie', 'eleza', 'fafanua', 'maana',
  27        'ipi', 'zipi', 'ngapi', 'kiasi', 'mara', 'bado', 'nipo', 'niko', 'ninaweza', 'naweza', 'unaweza', 'nifanye', 'nifanyeje',
  28        // English
  29        'the', 'is', 'are', 'and', 'of', 'to', 'in', 'for', 'what', 'how', 'why', 'when', 'where', 'who', 'which', 'can',
  30        'could', 'should', 'would', 'do', 'does', 'did', 'i', 'my', 'me', 'you', 'your', 'we', 'our', 'they', 'their', 'it',
  31        'this', 'that', 'these', 'with', 'from', 'about', 'please', 'help', 'need', 'want', 'explain', 'tell', 'describe',
  32        'define', 'have', 'has', 'been', 'was', 'were', 'will', 'not', 'but', 'or', 'also', 'there', 'here', 'now', 'a', 'an',
  33        'am', 'be', 'on', 'at', 'by', 'as', 'if', 'so', 'get', 'know', 'any', 'some', 'much', 'many',
  34        // Topic-level words that appear in almost every entry of a domain and therefore carry no signal.
  35        'sheria', 'kisheria', 'haki', 'legal', 'law', 'laws', 'rights', 'right', 'huduma', 'service', 'services',
  36        'serikali', 'government', 'tanzania', 'nchi', 'country', 'mwananchi', 'wananchi', 'citizen', 'citizens',
  37        'ofisi', 'office', 'taarifa', 'information', 'maelezo', 'swali', 'question',
  38    ];
  39
  40    /**
  41     * @return Collection<int, KnowledgeEntry> ordered by relevance, each with ->score
  42     */
  43    public function search(string $question, string $language, ?string $category = null, int $limit = 2): Collection
  44    {
  45        if ($limit <= 0) {
  46            return collect();
  47        }
  48
  49        $queryTokens = $this->tokens($question);
  50        if (empty($queryTokens)) {
  51            return collect();
  52        }
  53        $querySet = array_flip($queryTokens);
  54
  55        $entries = Cache::remember('huru.knowledge.active', 3600, fn () => KnowledgeEntry::query()
  56            ->where('is_active', true)
  57            ->get()
  58            ->map(function (KnowledgeEntry $e) {
  59                // Precompute token sets once per cache cycle.
  60                $e->setAttribute('_kw', array_flip($this->tokens(implode(' ', (array) $e->keywords))));
  61                $e->setAttribute('_title', array_flip($this->tokens($e->title)));
  62                $e->setAttribute('_body', array_flip($this->tokens(Str::limit($e->content, 1500, ''))));
  63
  64                return $e;
  65            }));
  66
  67        $scored = $entries->map(function (KnowledgeEntry $e) use ($querySet, $language, $category) {
  68            $score = 0;
  69            foreach ($querySet as $tok => $_) {
  70                if (isset($e->_kw[$tok])) {
  71                    $score += 3;
  72                }
  73                if (isset($e->_title[$tok])) {
  74                    $score += 2;
  75                }
  76                if (isset($e->_body[$tok])) {
  77                    $score += 1;
  78                }
  79            }
  80            if ($score === 0) {
  81                return null;
  82            }
  83            if ($category && $e->category === $category) {
  84                $score += 2;
  85            }
  86            if ($e->language !== $language) {
  87                $score = (int) ceil($score * 0.6);
  88            }
  89            $e->setAttribute('score', $score);
  90
  91            return $e;
  92        })->filter()->sortByDesc('score')->values();
  93
  94        return $scored->take($limit);
  95    }
  96
  97    /** @return string[] unique normalised, stemmed tokens */
  98    public function tokens(string $text): array
  99    {
 100        $text = Str::lower($text);
 101        $text = preg_replace('/[^\p{L}\p{N} ]+/u', ' ', $text);
 102        $words = preg_split('/\s+/', trim($text), -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY);
 103
 104        $out = [];
 105        foreach ($words as $w) {
 106            if (strlen($w) < 3 || in_array($w, self::STOP, true)) {
 107                continue;
 108            }
 109            $out[$w] = true;
 110            $stem = $this->stem($w);
 111            if ($stem !== $w && strlen($stem) >= 3) {
 112                $out[$stem] = true;
 113            }
 114        }
 115
 116        return array_keys($out);
 117    }
 118
 119    /**
 120     * Very small bilingual stemmer. Swahili: strip subject/tense prefixes and
 121     * common suffixes. English: strip plural/participle suffixes.
 122     */
 123    public function stem(string $w): string
 124    {
 125        $len = strlen($w);
 126        if ($len <= 4) {
 127            return $w;
 128        }
 129
 130        // Swahili prefixes (longest first)
 131        foreach (['nime', 'ume', 'ame', 'tume', 'mme', 'wame', 'nina', 'una', 'ana', 'tuna', 'mna', 'wana', 'nita', 'uta', 'ata', 'tuta', 'wata', 'nili', 'uli', 'ali', 'tuli', 'wali', 'kuna', 'kuwa', 'ku', 'wa', 'ki', 'vi', 'ma', 'mi', 'u'] as $p) {
 132            if (str_starts_with($w, $p) && strlen($w) - strlen($p) >= 4) {
 133                $w = substr($w, strlen($p));
 134                break;
 135            }
 136        }
 137        // Swahili suffixes
 138        foreach (['wa', 'ni', 'ji', 'ka', 'ia', 'ea'] as $s) {
 139            if (str_ends_with($w, $s) && strlen($w) - strlen($s) >= 4) {
 140                $w = substr($w, 0, -strlen($s));
 141                break;
 142            }
 143        }
 144        // English suffixes
 145        foreach (['ies', 'ing', 'ed', 'es', 's'] as $s) {
 146            if (str_ends_with($w, $s) && strlen($w) - strlen($s) >= 4) {
 147                $w = substr($w, 0, -strlen($s));
 148                if ($s === 'ies') {
 149                    $w .= 'y';
 150                }
 151                break;
 152            }
 153        }
 154
 155        return $w;
 156    }
 157}

42 app/Services/LanguageDetector.php 92 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services;

   5use Illuminate\Support\Str;

   7/**
   8 * Decides whether a message is Swahili ('sw') or English ('en').
   9 *
  10 * Strategy: weighted function-word lists first; if that is a tie, fall back
  11 * to a morphological heuristic (Swahili words overwhelmingly end in a vowel
  12 * and use characteristic prefixes). Returns null only for empty input.
  13 */
  14class LanguageDetector
  15{
  16    private const SW = [
  17        'na' => 1, 'ya' => 1, 'wa' => 1, 'za' => 1, 'la' => 1, 'cha' => 1, 'vya' => 1, 'kwa' => 2, 'ni' => 1, 'si' => 1,
  18        'nini' => 3, 'vipi' => 3, 'gani' => 3, 'wapi' => 3, 'lini' => 3, 'nani' => 3, 'mbona' => 3, 'kwanini' => 3, 'kwa nini' => 3,
  19        'je' => 2, 'habari' => 2, 'naomba' => 3, 'nataka' => 3, 'nahitaji' => 3, 'msaada' => 2, 'tafadhali' => 3, 'asante' => 3,
  20        'ninaweza' => 3, 'naweza' => 3, 'unaweza' => 3, 'nifanye' => 3, 'nifanyeje' => 3, 'kama' => 1, 'jinsi' => 2, 'hii' => 2,
  21        'hiyo' => 2, 'hili' => 2, 'hiki' => 2, 'yangu' => 2, 'wangu' => 2, 'langu' => 2, 'changu' => 2, 'zangu' => 2, 'yake' => 2,
  22        'wake' => 1, 'mimi' => 2, 'wewe' => 2, 'yeye' => 2, 'sisi' => 2, 'ninyi' => 2, 'wao' => 2, 'sana' => 2, 'tu' => 1,
  23        'lakini' => 2, 'au' => 1, 'pia' => 2, 'kuhusu' => 3, 'katika' => 3, 'kutoka' => 3, 'hadi' => 2, 'mpaka' => 2,
  24        'sasa' => 2, 'leo' => 2, 'kesho' => 2, 'jana' => 2, 'sheria' => 2, 'haki' => 1, 'mtoto' => 2, 'watu' => 2, 'mtu' => 2,
  25        'nchi' => 2, 'serikali' => 2, 'shilingi' => 2, 'pesa' => 2, 'kazi' => 1, 'shule' => 2, 'nyumba' => 2, 'ardhi' => 2,
  26        'ndoa' => 2, 'afya' => 2, 'dawa' => 2, 'polisi' => 1, 'eleza' => 3, 'nieleze' => 3, 'niambie' => 3, 'fafanua' => 3,
  27        'maana' => 2, 'ipi' => 2, 'zipi' => 2, 'ngapi' => 3, 'kiasi' => 2, 'mara' => 1, 'bado' => 2, 'tayari' => 2,
  28    ];
  29
  30    private const EN = [
  31        'the' => 2, 'is' => 1, 'are' => 1, 'and' => 1, 'of' => 1, 'to' => 1, 'in' => 1, 'for' => 1, 'what' => 3, 'how' => 3,
  32        'why' => 3, 'when' => 3, 'where' => 3, 'who' => 3, 'which' => 3, 'can' => 2, 'could' => 2, 'should' => 2, 'would' => 2,
  33        'do' => 1, 'does' => 2, 'did' => 2, 'i' => 1, 'my' => 2, 'me' => 2, 'you' => 2, 'your' => 2, 'we' => 2, 'our' => 2,
  34        'they' => 2, 'their' => 2, 'it' => 1, 'this' => 2, 'that' => 2, 'these' => 2, 'with' => 2, 'from' => 2, 'about' => 3,
  35        'please' => 3, 'help' => 2, 'need' => 2, 'want' => 2, 'explain' => 3, 'tell' => 2, 'describe' => 3, 'define' => 3,
  36        'difference' => 3, 'between' => 3, 'have' => 2, 'has' => 2, 'been' => 2, 'was' => 2, 'were' => 2, 'will' => 1,
  37        'not' => 2, 'but' => 2, 'or' => 1, 'also' => 2, 'there' => 2, 'here' => 2, 'now' => 2, 'today' => 2, 'tomorrow' => 2,
  38        'law' => 2, 'rights' => 2, 'child' => 2, 'people' => 2, 'person' => 2, 'country' => 2, 'government' => 2,
  39        'money' => 2, 'work' => 1, 'school' => 2, 'house' => 2, 'land' => 1, 'marriage' => 2, 'health' => 2, 'medicine' => 2,
  40        'much' => 2, 'many' => 2, 'if' => 2, 'because' => 3, 'get' => 1, 'know' => 2, 'think' => 2, 'any' => 2, 'some' => 2,
  41    ];
  42
  43    public function detect(string $text): ?string
  44    {
  45        $lower = Str::lower($text);
  46        $words = preg_split('/[^\p{L}\p{N}\']+/u', $lower, -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY);
  47
  48        if (empty($words)) {
  49            return null;
  50        }
  51
  52        $sw = 0;
  53        $en = 0;
  54        foreach ($words as $w) {
  55            $sw += self::SW[$w] ?? 0;
  56            $en += self::EN[$w] ?? 0;
  57        }
  58        if (str_contains($lower, 'kwa nini')) {
  59            $sw += 3;
  60        }
  61
  62        if ($sw !== $en) {
  63            return $sw > $en ? 'sw' : 'en';
  64        }
  65
  66        // Morphological tie-breaker.
  67        $vowelEnding = 0;
  68        $swPrefix = 0;
  69        foreach ($words as $w) {
  70            if (strlen($w) < 3) {
  71                continue;
  72            }
  73            if (preg_match('/[aeiou]$/', $w)) {
  74                $vowelEnding++;
  75            }
  76            if (preg_match('/^(ku|ni|u|a|tu|m|wa|ki|vi|ma|mi|ha|si|nime|ume|ame|tume|wame|nina|una|ana|tuna|wana|nita|uta|ata)/', $w)) {
  77                $swPrefix++;
  78            }
  79        }
  80        $considered = max(1, count(array_filter($words, fn ($w) => strlen($w) >= 3)));
  81        $ratio = $vowelEnding / $considered;
  82
  83        if ($ratio >= 0.7 || ($ratio >= 0.55 && $swPrefix / $considered >= 0.4)) {
  84            return 'sw';
  85        }
  86        if ($ratio <= 0.35) {
  87            return 'en';
  88        }
  89
  90        return null;
  91    }
  92}

43 app/Services/ModerationService.php 47 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services;
   4
   5use Illuminate\Support\Str;
   6
   7class ModerationService
   8{
   9    /** Abusive terms in Swahili and English. Matched as whole words. */
  10    protected array $forbiddenWords = [
  11        'pumbavu', 'mjinga', 'mshenzi', 'fala', 'mavi', 'mbwa wewe', 'kuma', 'mboro', 'shoga', 'malaya', 'kahaba', 'takataka',
  12        'fuck', 'fucking', 'shit', 'idiot', 'stupid', 'bastard', 'bitch', 'asshole', 'dick', 'pussy', 'whore', 'slut', 'nigger',
  13    ];
  14
  15    public function isAbusive(string $text): bool
  16    {
  17        $text = Str::lower($text);
  18
  19        foreach ($this->forbiddenWords as $word) {
  20            if (preg_match('/(?<![\p{L}\p{N}])' . preg_quote($word, '/') . '(?![\p{L}\p{N}])/u', $text)) {
  21                return true;
  22            }
  23        }
  24
  25        return false;
  26    }
  27
  28    public function getWarningMessage(string $language, int $abuseCount, int $limit): string
  29    {
  30        $left = max(0, $limit - $abuseCount);
  31
  32        if ($language === 'sw') {
  33            return "ONYO: Lugha ya matusi haikubaliki kwenye huduma hii. Hili ni onyo namba {$abuseCount}. Ukiendelea utafungiwa (nafasi zilizobaki: {$left}).";
  34        }
  35
  36        return "WARNING: Abusive language is not accepted on this service. This is warning #{$abuseCount}. Continued abuse will lead to a ban ({$left} chances left).";
  37    }
  38
  39    public function getBanMessage(string $language): string
  40    {
  41        if ($language === 'sw') {
  42            return 'HUDUMA IMEFUNGWA: Umefungiwa kutumia huduma hii kwa kukiuka masharti (lugha ya matusi). Wasiliana nasi ikiwa unaamini hili ni kosa.';
  43        }
  44
  45        return 'SERVICE BANNED: You have been blocked from this service for violating our terms (abusive language). Contact us if you believe this is a mistake.';
  46    }
  47}

44 app/Services/PromptEngine.php 347 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services;
   4
   5use App\Models\LocalResource;
   6use App\Models\PromptTemplate;
   7use App\Models\ReferenceContact;
   8use App\Support\Settings;
   9use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
  10use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;
  11use Illuminate\Support\Str;
  12
  13/**
  14 * Assembles the system instruction and conversation turns for the engine.
  15 *
  16 * Layers, in order:
  17 *   1. Persona for the detected topic and language (admin-editable template)
  18 *   2. Country context (date, currency, institutions, emergency numbers)
  19 *   3. Topic guidance (how this subject works in Tanzania)
  20 *   4. Citizen profile (name, region, detected location)
  21 *   5. Retrieved knowledge entries
  22 *   6. Local resources near the citizen
  23 *   7. National directory contacts relevant to the topic
  24 *   8. Output constraints for the channel (SMS vs web)
  25 */
  26class PromptEngine
  27{
  28    public function __construct(
  29        private readonly KnowledgeRetriever $retriever,
  30        private readonly TanzaniaContext $tz,
  31    ) {
  32    }
  33
  34    /**
  35     * @param array{
  36     *   question:string, language:string, category:string, channel:string,
  37     *   is_emergency?:bool, location?:?array, history?:array<int,array{role:string,text:string}>,
  38     *   user_name?:?string, user_region?:?string
  39     * } $ctx
  40     * @return array{system:string, turns:array, knowledge:Collection, resources:Collection, contacts:Collection}
  41     */
  42    public function build(array $ctx): array
  43    {
  44        $lang = $ctx['language'] === 'en' ? 'en' : 'sw';
  45        $category = $ctx['category'] ?: TopicClassifier::GENERAL;
  46        $channel = $ctx['channel'] ?? 'web';
  47        $question = trim($ctx['question']);
  48        $location = $ctx['location'] ?? null;
  49        $isEmergency = (bool) ($ctx['is_emergency'] ?? false);
  50
  51        $knowledge = $this->retriever->search($question, $lang, $category, Settings::int('knowledge_matches'));
  52        $resources = $this->findLocalResources($location, $category, $ctx['user_region'] ?? null);
  53        $contacts = $this->findContacts($category, $isEmergency);
  54
  55        $sections = [];
  56        $sections[] = $this->persona($category, $lang);
  57        $sections[] = $this->tz->localeBlock($lang);
  58        $sections[] = ($lang === 'en' ? "TOPIC RULES ({$this->label($category, $lang)}):\n" : "KANUNI ZA MADA ({$this->label($category, $lang)}):\n")
  59            . $this->tz->categoryGuidance($category, $lang);
  60        $sections[] = $this->profileBlock($ctx, $location, $lang);
  61        $sections[] = $this->knowledgeBlock($knowledge, $lang);
  62        $sections[] = $this->resourcesBlock($resources, $location, $lang);
  63        $sections[] = $this->contactsBlock($contacts, $isEmergency, $lang);
  64        $sections[] = $this->constraints($lang, $channel, $isEmergency);
  65
  66        $system = implode("\n\n", array_filter($sections));
  67
  68        $turns = [];
  69        foreach ($ctx['history'] ?? [] as $turn) {
  70            if (! empty($turn['text'])) {
  71                $turns[] = ['role' => $turn['role'], 'text' => $turn['text']];
  72            }
  73        }
  74        $turns[] = ['role' => 'user', 'text' => $question];
  75
  76        return [
  77            'system' => $system,
  78            'turns' => $turns,
  79            'knowledge' => $knowledge,
  80            'resources' => $resources,
  81            'contacts' => $contacts,
  82        ];
  83    }
  84
  85    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ blocks
  86
  87    private function persona(string $category, string $lang): string
  88    {
  89        $templates = Cache::remember('huru.templates.active', 3600, fn () => PromptTemplate::query()
  90            ->where('is_active', true)
  91            ->get()
  92            ->keyBy(fn (PromptTemplate $t) => "{$t->category}:{$t->language}"));
  93
  94        $tpl = $templates["{$category}:{$lang}"]
  95            ?? $templates[TopicClassifier::GENERAL . ":{$lang}"]
  96            ?? null;
  97
  98        $text = $tpl?->template ?: $this->defaultPersona($lang);
  99
 100        return str_replace(
 101            ['{app_name}', '{category}'],
 102            [Settings::get('app_name'), $this->label($category, $lang)],
 103            $text
 104        );
 105    }
 106
 107    private function defaultPersona(string $lang): string
 108    {
 109        return $lang === 'en'
 110            ? 'You are {app_name}, a trusted everyday helper for citizens of Tanzania. You answer any question, on any subject, with accurate, practical, locally relevant guidance. You speak as a knowledgeable, respectful Tanzanian neighbour would: clear, direct, warm, never condescending.'
 111            : 'Wewe ni {app_name}, msaidizi wa kuaminika wa kila siku kwa wananchi wa Tanzania. Unajibu swali lolote, la mada yoyote, kwa usahihi, kwa vitendo na kwa muktadha wa Tanzania. Unazungumza kama jirani Mtanzania mwenye maarifa na heshima: wazi, moja kwa moja, mkarimu, bila kudharau.';
 112    }
 113
 114    private function profileBlock(array $ctx, ?array $location, string $lang): string
 115    {
 116        $lines = [];
 117        if (! empty($ctx['user_name'])) {
 118            $lines[] = ($lang === 'en' ? '- Name: ' : '- Jina: ') . $ctx['user_name'];
 119        }
 120        if ($location) {
 121            $place = array_filter([
 122                $location['area'] ? Str::title($location['area']) : null,
 123                $location['district'] ? Str::title($location['district']) : null,
 124                $location['region'] ? Str::title($location['region']) : null,
 125            ]);
 126            $lines[] = ($lang === 'en' ? '- Location mentioned in this question: ' : '- Mahali alipotaja kwenye swali hili: ') . implode(', ', $place);
 127        } elseif (! empty($ctx['user_region'])) {
 128            $lines[] = ($lang === 'en' ? '- Home region on file: ' : '- Mkoa wake uliohifadhiwa: ') . Str::title($ctx['user_region']);
 129        }
 130        if (empty($lines)) {
 131            return '';
 132        }
 133
 134        return ($lang === 'en' ? "ABOUT THE CITIZEN:\n" : "KUHUSU MWANANCHI:\n") . implode("\n", $lines);
 135    }
 136
 137    private function knowledgeBlock(Collection $knowledge, string $lang): string
 138    {
 139        if ($knowledge->isEmpty()) {
 140            return $lang === 'en'
 141                ? 'VERIFIED LOCAL KNOWLEDGE: none matched this question. Rely on your general knowledge of Tanzania and say clearly when something should be confirmed with the responsible office.'
 142                : 'MAARIFA YALIYOTHIBITISHWA: hakuna yaliyolingana na swali hili. Tumia maarifa yako ya jumla kuhusu Tanzania na sema wazi pale ambapo jambo linapaswa kuthibitishwa na ofisi husika.';
 143        }
 144
 145        $out = $lang === 'en'
 146            ? "VERIFIED LOCAL KNOWLEDGE (prefer this over your own memory when they conflict):\n"
 147            : "MAARIFA YALIYOTHIBITISHWA (yape kipaumbele kuliko kumbukumbu yako yakipingana):\n";
 148
 149        foreach ($knowledge as $e) {
 150            $out .= "[{$e->title}]\n" . trim($e->content) . "\n";
 151            if ($e->source) {
 152                $out .= ($lang === 'en' ? 'Source: ' : 'Chanzo: ') . $e->source . "\n";
 153            }
 154            $out .= "\n";
 155        }
 156
 157        return trim($out);
 158    }
 159
 160    private function resourcesBlock(Collection $resources, ?array $location, string $lang): string
 161    {
 162        if ($resources->isEmpty()) {
 163            return '';
 164        }
 165
 166        $where = $location ? Str::title($location['district'] ?? $location['region']) : '';
 167        $out = $lang === 'en'
 168            ? "SERVICES NEAR THE CITIZEN" . ($where ? " ({$where})" : '') . " (mention the most relevant ones by name with their contact):\n"
 169            : "HUDUMA ZILIZO KARIBU NA MWANANCHI" . ($where ? " ({$where})" : '') . " (taja zinazofaa zaidi kwa jina na mawasiliano yake):\n";
 170
 171        foreach ($resources as $r) {
 172            $out .= "- {$r->name} (" . (LocalResource::CATEGORIES[$r->category] ?? $r->category) . '; ' . Str::title($r->district ?: $r->region) . ")";
 173            $bits = array_filter([$r->location, $r->phone ? ('Simu/Phone: ' . $r->phone) : null, $r->email ? ('Email: ' . $r->email) : null]);
 174            if ($bits) {
 175                $out .= ': ' . implode('; ', $bits);
 176            }
 177            $out .= "\n";
 178        }
 179
 180        return trim($out);
 181    }
 182
 183    private function contactsBlock(Collection $contacts, bool $isEmergency, string $lang): string
 184    {
 185        if ($contacts->isEmpty()) {
 186            return '';
 187        }
 188
 189        $out = $lang === 'en'
 190            ? "OFFICIAL NATIONAL CONTACTS (use only when they help the citizen act; do not pad ordinary answers with them):\n"
 191            : "MAWASILIANO RASMI YA KITAIFA (tumia tu pale yanapomsaidia mwananchi kuchukua hatua; usiyajaze kwenye majibu ya kawaida):\n";
 192
 193        foreach ($contacts as $c) {
 194            $bits = array_filter([$c->phone, $c->alt_phone, $c->email, $c->website]);
 195            $out .= '- ' . $c->name . ($c->is_emergency ? ($lang === 'en' ? ' [EMERGENCY]' : ' [DHARURA]') : '')
 196                . ($bits ? ': ' . implode(' / ', $bits) : '')
 197                . ($c->description($lang) ? ' - ' . $c->description($lang) : '')
 198                . "\n";
 199        }
 200
 201        if ($isEmergency) {
 202            $out .= $lang === 'en'
 203                ? "\nTHIS LOOKS LIKE AN EMERGENCY: put the correct emergency number and the single most important immediate action in the FIRST line of your reply."
 204                : "\nHILI LINAONEKANA KUWA DHARURA: weka namba sahihi ya dharura na hatua moja muhimu zaidi ya haraka kwenye MSTARI WA KWANZA wa jibu lako.";
 205        }
 206
 207        return trim($out);
 208    }
 209
 210    private function constraints(string $lang, string $channel, bool $isEmergency): string
 211    {
 212        $maxWords = $channel === 'sms' ? Settings::int('sms_max_words') : Settings::int('web_max_words');
 213        $signature = trim((string) Settings::get($lang === 'en' ? 'signature_en' : 'signature_sw'));
 214
 215        if ($lang === 'en') {
 216            $rules = [
 217                'Reply ONLY in the language of the citizen\'s latest message (English here). If they switch language, switch with them.',
 218                "Keep it under {$maxWords} words. " . ($channel === 'sms'
 219                    ? 'This is an SMS: short sentences, the most useful fact first, no lists longer than 4 items.'
 220                    : 'Use short paragraphs; a brief numbered list is fine for steps.'),
 221                'Plain text only. No markdown, no asterisks, no emoji, no headings.',
 222                'No greetings, no "as an assistant", no repeating the question. Start with the answer.',
 223                'Be honest: if you do not know or the information may have changed (fees, officials, deadlines), say so and name the office or website that can confirm.',
 224                'Never invent phone numbers, prices, laws or names of officials. Use only numbers given in this instruction or well-established public ones.',
 225                'Give no medical dosages, no legal representation, no political campaigning, no religious judgement.',
 226                'If the citizen asked a follow-up, use the earlier conversation turns to keep continuity.',
 227            ];
 228            if ($isEmergency) {
 229                array_unshift($rules, 'EMERGENCY: first line must be the emergency number and the immediate action.');
 230            }
 231            $out = "OUTPUT RULES:\n- " . implode("\n- ", $rules);
 232            if ($signature !== '') {
 233                $out .= "\n- End with exactly this line: \"{$signature}\"";
 234            }
 235
 236            return $out;
 237        }
 238
 239        $rules = [
 240            'Jibu TU kwa lugha ya ujumbe wa mwisho wa mwananchi (Kiswahili hapa). Akibadili lugha, badilika naye.',
 241            "Usizidi maneno {$maxWords}. " . ($channel === 'sms'
 242                ? 'Huu ni ujumbe wa SMS: sentensi fupi, jambo muhimu zaidi kwanza, orodha isizidi vipengele 4.'
 243                : 'Tumia aya fupi; orodha fupi ya namba inafaa kwa hatua.'),
 244            'Maandishi ya kawaida tu. Bila markdown, bila nyota (*), bila emoji, bila vichwa vya habari.',
 245            'Bila salamu, bila "kama msaidizi", bila kurudia swali. Anza na jibu.',
 246            'Kuwa mkweli: usipojua au taarifa inaweza kuwa imebadilika (ada, viongozi, tarehe za mwisho), sema hivyo na taja ofisi au tovuti inayoweza kuthibitisha.',
 247            'Usizue kamwe namba za simu, bei, sheria au majina ya viongozi. Tumia tu namba zilizotolewa kwenye maelekezo haya au zinazojulikana wazi.',
 248            'Usitoe dozi za dawa, usijifanye wakili, usifanye kampeni za kisiasa, usihukumu dini.',
 249            'Kama mwananchi ameuliza swali la kufuatilia, tumia mazungumzo ya awali kuendeleza mtiririko.',
 250        ];
 251        if ($isEmergency) {
 252            array_unshift($rules, 'DHARURA: mstari wa kwanza lazima uwe namba ya dharura na hatua ya haraka.');
 253        }
 254        $out = "KANUNI ZA JIBU:\n- " . implode("\n- ", $rules);
 255        if ($signature !== '') {
 256            $out .= "\n- Malizia kwa mstari huu haswa: \"{$signature}\"";
 257        }
 258
 259        return $out;
 260    }

 262    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ lookups

 264    private function findLocalResources(?array $location, string $category, ?string $userRegion): Collection
 265    {
 266        $region = $location['region'] ?? $userRegion;
 267        if (! $region) {
 268            return collect();
 269        }
 270
 271        $categoryMap = [
 272            'legal' => ['legal_aid', 'police'],
 273            'family' => ['police', 'legal_aid', 'government'],
 274            'health' => ['health'],
 275            'agriculture' => ['agriculture'],
 276            'government' => ['government'],
 277            'education' => ['education'],
 278            'finance' => ['finance'],
 279            'business' => ['finance', 'government'],
 280            'employment' => ['government', 'legal_aid'],
 281        ];
 282        $wanted = $categoryMap[$category] ?? null;
 283
 284        $query = LocalResource::query()->where('is_active', true)
 285            ->whereRaw('LOWER(region) = ?', [Str::lower($region)]);
 286        if ($wanted) {
 287            $query->whereIn('category', $wanted);
 288        }
 289
 290        $all = $query->get();
 291        if ($all->isEmpty()) {
 292            return collect();
 293        }
 294
 295        $district = $location['district'] ?? null;
 296        $area = $location['area'] ?? null;
 297
 298        return $all->sortByDesc(function (LocalResource $r) use ($district, $area) {
 299            $score = 0;
 300            if ($district && Str::lower((string) $r->district) === $district) {
 301                $score += 5;
 302            }
 303            if ($area && str_contains(Str::lower((string) $r->location . ' ' . $r->ward), $area)) {
 304                $score += 8;
 305            }
 306
 307            return $score;
 308        })->take(4)->values();
 309    }
 310
 311    private function findContacts(string $category, bool $isEmergency): Collection
 312    {
 313        $contacts = Cache::remember('huru.contacts.active', 3600, fn () => ReferenceContact::query()
 314            ->where('is_active', true)
 315            ->orderBy('sort_order')
 316            ->get());
 317
 318        $related = [
 319            'legal' => ['legal', 'police'],
 320            'family' => ['family', 'police', 'legal'],
 321            'health' => ['health'],
 322            'agriculture' => ['agriculture'],
 323            'education' => ['education'],
 324            'government' => ['government'],
 325            'finance' => ['finance'],
 326            'business' => ['business', 'finance'],
 327            'employment' => ['employment', 'legal'],
 328            'technology' => ['technology'],
 329            'transport' => ['transport', 'police'],
 330            'general' => [],
 331        ];
 332        $wanted = $related[$category] ?? [];
 333
 334        $selected = $contacts->filter(fn (ReferenceContact $c) => in_array($c->category, $wanted, true));
 335
 336        if ($isEmergency) {
 337            $selected = $contacts->filter(fn (ReferenceContact $c) => $c->is_emergency)->merge($selected);
 338        }
 339
 340        return $selected->unique('id')->take(8)->values();
 341    }
 342
 343    private function label(string $category, string $lang): string
 344    {
 345        return TopicClassifier::label($category, $lang);
 346    }

45 app/Services/SmsService.php 93 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Services;
   4
   5use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
   6use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
   7
   8/**
   9 * Africa's Talking SMS over their REST API, through Laravel's HTTP client
  10 * so the global TLS settings, retries and timeouts apply.
  11 */
  12class SmsService
  13{
  14    protected ?string $username;
  15    protected ?string $apiKey;
  16    protected ?string $from;
  17
  18    public function __construct()
  19    {
  20        $this->username = config('services.at.username');
  21        $this->apiKey = config('services.at.api_key');
  22        $this->from = config('services.at.from');
  23    }
  24
  25    public function isConfigured(): bool
  26    {
  27        return ! empty($this->username) && ! empty($this->apiKey);
  28    }
  29
  30    public function endpoint(): string
  31    {
  32        return $this->username === 'sandbox'
  33            ? 'https://api.sandbox.africastalking.com/version1/messaging'
  34            : 'https://api.africastalking.com/version1/messaging';
  35    }
  36
  37    /**
  38     * @return array{status:string, data?:mixed, message?:string}
  39     */
  40    public function send(string $to, string $message): array
  41    {
  42        if (! $this->isConfigured()) {
  43            Log::warning('SMS not sent: Africa\'s Talking credentials missing.', ['to' => $this->mask($to)]);
  44
  45            return ['status' => 'skipped', 'message' => 'sms_not_configured'];
  46        }
  47
  48        $payload = ['username' => $this->username, 'to' => $to, 'message' => $message];
  49        if ($this->from) {
  50            $payload['from'] = $this->from;
  51        }
  52
  53        try {
  54            $response = Http::timeout(20)
  55                ->retry(2, 500, throw: false)
  56                ->withHeaders(['apiKey' => $this->apiKey, 'Accept' => 'application/json'])
  57                ->asForm()
  58                ->post($this->endpoint(), $payload);
  59
  60            $data = $response->json();
  61            $recipient = $data['SMSMessageData']['Recipients'][0] ?? null;
  62            $status = $recipient['status'] ?? ($data['SMSMessageData']['Message'] ?? 'HTTP ' . $response->status());
  63
  64            if (! $response->successful() || ! $recipient || ! in_array($recipient['statusCode'] ?? 0, [100, 101, 102], true)) {
  65                Log::error('SMS send failed', ['to' => $this->mask($to), 'status' => $status, 'http' => $response->status()]);
  66
  67                return ['status' => 'error', 'message' => (string) $status, 'data' => $data];
  68            }
  69
  70            Log::info('SMS sent', ['to' => $this->mask($to), 'chars' => strlen($message), 'cost' => $recipient['cost'] ?? null]);
  71
  72            return ['status' => 'success', 'data' => $data];
  73        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
  74            Log::error('SMS send error: ' . $e->getMessage());
  75
  76            return ['status' => 'error', 'message' => $e->getMessage()];
  77        }
  78    }
  79
  80    public function sendOtp(string $to, string $code, string $language, string $appName, int $ttlMinutes): array
  81    {
  82        $text = $language === 'en'
  83            ? "{$appName}: your verification code is {$code}. It expires in {$ttlMinutes} minutes. Do not share it with anyone."
  84            : "{$appName}: namba yako ya uthibitisho ni {$code}. Inaisha baada ya dakika {$ttlMinutes}. Usimpe mtu yeyote.";
  85
  86        return $this->send($to, $text);
  87    }
  88
  89    private function mask(string $phone): string
  90    {
  91        return strlen($phone) > 6 ? substr($phone, 0, 4) . '****' . substr($phone, -3) : '****';
  92    }
  93}

 106    /**
 107     * Detect region / district / area mentioned in free text.
 108     *
 109     * @return array{region:?string,district:?string,area:?string}|null
 110     */
 111    public function detectLocation(string $text): ?array
 112    {
 113        $norm = ' ' . trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', preg_replace('/[^\p{L}\p{N} ]+/u', ' ', Str::lower($text)))) . ' ';
 114
 115        $result = ['region' => null, 'district' => null, 'area' => null];
 116
 117        foreach (self::AREAS as $area => [$district, $region]) {
 118            if (str_contains($norm, " {$area} ")) {
 119                $result = ['region' => $region, 'district' => $district, 'area' => $area];
 120                break;
 121            }
 122        }
 123
 124        if (! $result['district']) {
 125            foreach (self::DISTRICTS as $district => $region) {
 126                if (str_contains($norm, " {$district} ")) {
 127                    $result['district'] = $district;
 128                    $result['region'] = $region;
 129                    break;
 130                }
 131            }
 132        }
 133
 134        if (! $result['region']) {
 135            foreach (self::REGIONS as $region) {
 136                if (str_contains($norm, " {$region} ")) {
 137                    $result['region'] = $region;
 138                    break;
 139                }
 140            }
 141        }
 142
 143        if (! $result['region']) {
 144            foreach (self::REGION_ALIASES as $alias => $region) {
 145                if (str_contains($norm, " {$alias} ")) {
 146                    $result['region'] = $region;
 147                    break;
 148                }
 149            }
 150        }
 151
 152        return $result['region'] ? $result : null;
 153    }
 154
 155    public function now(): Carbon
 156    {
 157        return Carbon::now(self::TIMEZONE);
 158    }
 159
 160    /** Country facts the engine must assume, in the citizen's language. */
 161    public function localeBlock(string $language): string
 162    {
 163        $now = $this->now();
 164
 165        if ($language === 'en') {
 166            return "COUNTRY CONTEXT (assume these facts):\n"
 167                . '- Country: United Republic of Tanzania (Mainland and Zanzibar). Today is ' . $now->format('l, j F Y') . ', time ' . $now->format('H:i') . " East Africa Time.\n"
 168                . "- Capital: Dodoma (seat of government and parliament). Commercial capital and largest city: Dar es Salaam.\n"
 169                . "- Currency: Tanzanian Shilling (TZS). Never quote prices in other currencies unless asked.\n"
 170                . "- Languages: Swahili (national) and English (official). Many citizens read Swahili more comfortably.\n"
 171                . "- Mobile money (M-Pesa, Tigo Pesa/Mixx by Yas, Airtel Money, HaloPesa) is the everyday payment method. Government payments go through GePG control numbers.\n"
 172                . "- Local government chain: Village/Mtaa Executive Officer, Ward Executive Officer (WEO), District Council/Commissioner, Regional Commissioner. Most everyday problems start at the ward office.\n"
 173                . "- Legal system: common law with the Constitution of 1977; primary courts, district courts, High Court, Court of Appeal. Ward tribunals and District Land and Housing Tribunals handle local disputes.\n"

 226    public static function regionLabel(?string $region): ?string
 227    {
 228        return $region ? Str::title($region) : null;
 229    }
 230
 231    private function swahiliDate(Carbon $date): string
 232    {
 233        $days = ['Monday' => 'Jumatatu', 'Tuesday' => 'Jumanne', 'Wednesday' => 'Jumatano', 'Thursday' => 'Alhamisi', 'Friday' => 'Ijumaa', 'Saturday' => 'Jumamosi', 'Sunday' => 'Jumapili'];
 234        $months = [1 => 'Januari', 'Februari', 'Machi', 'Aprili', 'Mei', 'Juni', 'Julai', 'Agosti', 'Septemba', 'Oktoba', 'Novemba', 'Desemba'];
 235
 236        return $days[$date->format('l')] . ', ' . $date->day . ' ' . $months[$date->month] . ' ' . $date->year;
 237    }

  12 * right contacts. It also flags emergencies so safety numbers are surfaced

 185    /** Patterns that indicate a life-threatening or urgent situation. */
 186    private const EMERGENCY = [
 187        'dharura', 'emergency', 'ajali', 'accident', 'moto', 'fire', 'anakufa', 'dying', 'amezimia', 'unconscious',
 188        'kujiua', 'suicide', 'nataka kufa', 'kill myself', 'ananipiga sasa', 'anataka kuniua', 'damu nyingi', 'bleeding',
 189        'sumu', 'poison', 'nyoka ameniuma', 'snake bite', 'kupumua' , 'breathe', 'mafuriko', 'flood', 'amepotea', 'missing child',
 190        'mtoto amepotea', 'kutekwa', 'kidnapped', 'wezi wameingia', 'robbery', 'haraka sana', 'help me now',
 191    ];
 192
 193    /**
 194     * @return array{category:string, confidence:float, scores:array<string,int>, is_emergency:bool}
 195     */
 196    public function classify(string $text): array
 197    {
 198        $normalized = $this->normalize($text);
 199        $words = array_filter(explode(' ', $normalized));
 200        $wordSet = array_flip($words);
 201
 202        $scores = [];
 203        foreach (self::KEYWORDS as $category => $keywords) {
 204            $score = 0;
 205            foreach ($keywords as $keyword => $weight) {
 206                if (str_contains($keyword, ' ') || str_contains($keyword, '-') || str_contains($keyword, "'")) {
 207                    if (str_contains($normalized, $keyword)) {
 208                        $score += $weight + 1; // phrases are strong signals
 209                    }
 210                } elseif (isset($wordSet[$keyword])) {
 211                    $score += $weight;
 212                }
 213            }
 214            if ($score > 0) {
 215                $scores[$category] = $score;
 216            }
 217        }
 218
 219        arsort($scores);
 220        $top = array_key_first($scores);
 221        $topScore = $top ? $scores[$top] : 0;
 222        $total = array_sum($scores) ?: 1;
 223
 224        $category = ($top && $topScore >= 2) ? $top : self::GENERAL;
 225        $confidence = $top ? round($topScore / $total, 2) : 0.0;
 226
 227        return [
 228            'category' => $category,
 229            'confidence' => $confidence,
 230            'scores' => $scores,
 231            'is_emergency' => $this->isEmergency($normalized),
 232        ];
 233    }
 234
 235    public function isEmergency(string $normalizedText): bool
 236    {
 237        foreach (self::EMERGENCY as $needle) {
 238            if (str_contains($normalizedText, $needle)) {
 239                return true;
 240            }
 241        }
 242
 243        return false;
 244    }
 245
 246    public static function label(string $category, string $language = 'sw'): string
 247    {
 248        $labels = self::CATEGORIES[$category] ?? self::CATEGORIES[self::GENERAL];
 249
 250        return $labels[$language === 'en' ? 'en' : 'sw'];
 251    }
 252
 253    public static function keys(): array
 254    {
 255        return array_keys(self::CATEGORIES);
 256    }
 257
 258    private function normalize(string $text): string
 259    {
 260        $text = Str::lower($text);
 261        $text = preg_replace('/[^\p{L}\p{N}\'\- ]+/u', ' ', $text);
 262        $text = preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $text);
 263
 264        return ' ' . trim($text) . ' ';
 265    }
 266}

48 app/Support/Phone.php 55 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace App\Support;
   4
   5class Phone
   6{
   7    /**
   8     * Normalise a phone number to E.164, assuming Tanzania (+255) when no
   9     * country code is given. Returns null when the input cannot be a number.
  10     */
  11    public static function normalize(?string $input): ?string
  12    {
  13        if ($input === null) {
  14            return null;
  15        }
  16        $raw = preg_replace('/[^\d+]/', '', trim($input));
  17        if ($raw === '' || $raw === '+') {
  18            return null;
  19        }
  20
  21        if (str_starts_with($raw, '+')) {
  22            $digits = substr($raw, 1);
  23        } elseif (str_starts_with($raw, '00')) {
  24            $digits = substr($raw, 2);
  25        } elseif (str_starts_with($raw, '255')) {
  26            $digits = $raw;
  27        } elseif (str_starts_with($raw, '0')) {
  28            $digits = '255' . substr($raw, 1);
  29        } elseif (strlen($raw) === 9 && in_array($raw[0], ['6', '7'], true)) {
  30            $digits = '255' . $raw;
  31        } else {
  32            $digits = $raw;
  33        }
  34
  35        if (! preg_match('/^\d{10,15}$/', $digits)) {
  36            return null;
  37        }
  38
  39        return '+' . $digits;
  40    }
  41
  42    public static function isTanzanian(string $e164): bool
  43    {
  44        return (bool) preg_match('/^\+255[67]\d{8}$/', $e164);
  45    }
  46
  47    public static function mask(?string $e164): string
  48    {
  49        if (! $e164 || strlen($e164) < 7) {
  50            return '****';
  51        }
  52
  53        return substr($e164, 0, 4) . ' *** ' . substr($e164, -3);
  54    }
  55}

 136    public static function groups(): array
 137    {
 138        $groups = [];
 139        foreach (self::schema() as $key => $def) {
 140            $groups[$def['group']][$key] = $def;
 141        }
 142
 143        return $groups;
 144    }
 145
 146    public static function isKnown(string $key): bool
 147    {
 148        return array_key_exists($key, self::schema());
 149    }
 150
 151    /** @return array<string,string|null> */
 152    public static function all(): array
 153    {
 154        $stored = Cache::remember(self::CACHE_KEY, 3600, function () {
 155            try {
 156                return SystemSetting::query()->pluck('value', 'key')->all();
 157            } catch (\Throwable) {
 158                return [];
 159            }
 160        });
 161
 162        $out = [];
 163        foreach (self::schema() as $key => $def) {
 164            $out[$key] = array_key_exists($key, $stored) && $stored[$key] !== null
 165                ? $stored[$key]
 166                : $def['default'];
 167        }
 168
 169        return $out;
 170    }
 171
 172    public static function get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
 173    {
 174        $all = self::all();
 175        if (array_key_exists($key, $all)) {
 176            return $all[$key];
 177        }
 178
 179        return $default ?? (self::schema()[$key]['default'] ?? null);
 180    }
 181
 182    public static function bool(string $key): bool
 183    {
 184        return in_array((string) self::get($key), ['1', 'true', 'on', 'yes'], true);
 185    }
 186
 187    public static function int(string $key): int
 188    {
 189        return (int) self::get($key);
 190    }
 191
 192    public static function float(string $key): float
 193    {
 194        return (float) self::get($key);
 195    }
 196
 197    public static function set(string $key, mixed $value): void
 198    {
 199        if (! self::isKnown($key)) {
 200            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Unknown setting [{$key}]");
 201        }
 202
 203        SystemSetting::query()->updateOrCreate(['key' => $key], ['value' => $value === null ? null : (string) $value]);
 204        self::flush();
 205    }
 206
 207    public static function flush(): void
 208    {
 209        Cache::forget(self::CACHE_KEY);
 210    }
 211
 212    /** Validation rules for the whole schema, used by the admin form. */
 213    public static function rules(): array
 214    {
 215        $rules = [];
 216        foreach (self::schema() as $key => $def) {
 217            $rules[$key] = $def['rules'];
 218        }
 219
 220        return $rules;
 221    }
 222}

55 database/factories/UserFactory.php 40 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace Database\Factories;
   4
   5use App\Models\User;
   6use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
   7use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
   8use Illuminate\Support\Str;
   9
  10/**
  11 * @extends Factory<User>
  12 */
  13class UserFactory extends Factory
  14{
  15    protected static ?string $password;
  16
  17    public function definition(): array
  18    {
  19        return [
  20            'name' => fake()->name(),
  21            'phone_number' => '+2557' . fake()->unique()->numerify('########'),
  22            'email' => null,
  23            'password' => null,
  24            'role' => User::ROLE_USER,
  25            'is_banned' => false,
  26            'abuse_count' => 0,
  27            'opted_out' => false,
  28            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
  29        ];
  30    }
  31
  32    public function admin(): static
  33    {
  34        return $this->state(fn () => [
  35            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
  36            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
  37            'role' => User::ROLE_ADMIN,
  38        ]);
  39    }
  40}

62 database/seeders/AdminUserSeeder.php 44 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace Database\Seeders;
   4
   5use App\Models\User;
   6use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
   7use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
   8use Illuminate\Support\Str;
   9
  10class AdminUserSeeder extends Seeder
  11{
  12    public function run(): void
  13    {
  14        $email = env('ADMIN_EMAIL', 'admin@huru.local');
  15        $password = env('ADMIN_PASSWORD');
  16        $generated = false;
  17
  18        if (! $password) {
  19            $password = Str::password(14, symbols: false);
  20            $generated = true;
  21        }
  22
  23        $admin = User::query()->where('email', $email)->first();
  24
  25        if (! $admin) {
  26            User::query()->create([
  27                'name' => env('ADMIN_NAME', 'Huru SMS Admin'),
  28                'email' => $email,
  29                'password' => Hash::make($password),
  30                'role' => User::ROLE_ADMIN,
  31                'preferred_language' => 'en',
  32            ]);
  33            $this->command?->info("Admin created: {$email}");
  34            if ($generated) {
  35                $this->command?->warn("Generated admin password (set ADMIN_PASSWORD in .env to choose your own): {$password}");
  36            }
  37        } else {
  38            if ($admin->role !== User::ROLE_ADMIN) {
  39                $admin->forceFill(['role' => User::ROLE_ADMIN])->save();
  40            }
  41            $this->command?->info("Admin already exists: {$email}");
  42        }
  43    }
  44}

  12            SettingsSeeder::class,
  13            AdminUserSeeder::class,
  14            PromptTemplateSeeder::class,
  15            KnowledgeSeeder::class,
  16            ReferenceContactSeeder::class,
  17            LocalResourceSeeder::class,
  18            CommunitySeeder::class,
  19        ]);
  20    }

 328                'content' => 'Tanganyika gained independence on 9 December 1961 (first President Mwalimu Julius Nyerere); the Zanzibar Revolution was on 12 January 1964; the two united on 26 April 1964 to form the United Republic of Tanzania. 69 database/seeders/SettingsSeeder.php 25 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace Database\Seeders;
   4
   5use App\Models\SystemSetting;
   6use App\Support\Settings;
   7use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
   8
   9class SettingsSeeder extends Seeder
  10{
  11    public function run(): void
  12    {
  13        foreach (Settings::schema() as $key => $def) {
  14            SystemSetting::query()->firstOrCreate(['key' => $key], ['value' => $def['default']]);
  15        }
  16
  17        // Environment can pre-fill the operator contact.
  18        if ($email = env('ADMIN_EMAIL')) {
  19            SystemSetting::query()->updateOrCreate(['key' => 'contact_email'], ['value' => $email]);
  20        }
  21
  22        Settings::flush();
  23        $this->command?->info('Settings seeded (' . count(Settings::schema()) . ' keys).');
  24    }
  25}

74 public/sw.js 40 lines

   1/* Huru SMS service worker: app shell cached, API never cached, offline fallback page. */
   2const VERSION = 'huru-v1';
   3const SHELL = ['/', '/chat', '/offline', '/logo.svg', '/manifest.json'];
   4
   5self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
   6    event.waitUntil(caches.open(VERSION).then((c) => c.addAll(SHELL)).then(() => self.skipWaiting()));
   7});
   8
   9self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  10    event.waitUntil(
  11        caches.keys().then((keys) => Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k !== VERSION).map((k) => caches.delete(k)))).then(() => self.clients.claim())
  12    );
  13});
  14
  15self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  16    const req = event.request;
  17    const url = new URL(req.url);
  18
  19    if (req.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== self.location.origin) return;
  20    // Never cache dynamic data or the admin panel.
  21    if (url.pathname.startsWith('/chat/') || url.pathname.startsWith('/admin') || url.pathname.startsWith('/api') || url.pathname.startsWith('/community')) return;
  22
  23    if (req.mode === 'navigate') {
  24        event.respondWith(
  25            fetch(req)
  26                .then((res) => { const copy = res.clone(); caches.open(VERSION).then((c) => c.put(req, copy)); return res; })
  27                .catch(() => caches.match(req).then((hit) => hit || caches.match('/offline')))
  28        );
  29        return;
  30    }
  31
  32    event.respondWith(
  33        caches.match(req).then((hit) => hit || fetch(req).then((res) => {
  34            if (res.ok && (url.pathname.startsWith('/build/') || /\.(svg|png|ico|css|js|woff2?)$/.test(url.pathname))) {
  35                const copy = res.clone(); caches.open(VERSION).then((c) => c.put(req, copy));
  36            }
  37            return res;
  38        }))
  39    );
  40});

  36        input,select,textarea{width:100%;padding:12px 14px;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;font:inherit;font-size:1rem;background:#fff}
  37        input:focus,select:focus,textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--green)}
  38        .row{display:flex;gap:10px}
  39        .btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;padding:12px 16px;border-radius:8px;border:2px solid transparent;font:inherit;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;font-size:.95rem}
  40        .btn-green{background:var(--green);color:#fff;width:100%}
  41        .btn-green:hover{background:var(--green-dark)}
  42        .btn-green:disabled{opacity:.6;cursor:wait}
  43        .btn-ghost{background:#fff;border-color:var(--border);color:var(--muted)}
  44        .btn-link{background:none;border:none;color:var(--blue);font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;font:inherit;padding:6px 0}
  45        .err{background:#fef2f2;border:1px solid #fecaca;color:var(--danger);padding:10px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:.88rem;font-weight:600;margin:10px 0}
  46        .ok{background:var(--green-bg);border:1px solid var(--green-border);color:var(--green-dark);padding:10px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:.88rem;font-weight:600;margin:10px 0}
  47        .small{font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted);margin-top:10px}

  55        .tag{font-size:.75rem;font-weight:700;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--green-bg);color:var(--green-dark);border:1px solid var(--green-border)}
  56        .ibtn{background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:7px 10px;font:inherit;font-size:.8rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);cursor:pointer;text-decoration:none}
  57        .ibtn:hover{border-color:var(--blue);color:var(--blue)}
  58        .offline{background:#fffbeb;border-bottom:1px solid #fde68a;color:#92400e;font-size:.85rem;padding:8px 14px;font-weight:600}
  59        /* filters */

  69        .sugg{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-top:10px}
  70        .sugg button{background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:999px;padding:6px 11px;font:inherit;font-size:.8rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--blue);cursor:pointer}
  71        .sugg button:hover{border-color:var(--blue)}
  72        .msg{max-width:84%;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:14px;font-size:.95rem;line-height:1.5;white-space:pre-wrap;word-wrap:break-word;position:relative}
  73        .msg.in{align-self:flex-end;background:var(--green);color:#fff;border-bottom-right-radius:4px}
  74        .msg.out{align-self:flex-start;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-bottom-left-radius:4px}
  75        .msg.sys{align-self:center;background:var(--blue-bg);border:1px solid #bfdbfe;color:var(--blue-dark);font-size:.85rem;max-width:95%}
  76        .meta{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-top:6px;font-size:.72rem;opacity:.85}
  77        .msg.in .meta{justify-content:flex-end}
  78        .msg.out .meta{color:var(--muted)}
  79        .cat{background:var(--green-bg);color:var(--green-dark);border:1px solid var(--green-border);padding:1px 7px;border-radius:999px;font-weight:700}
  80        .fb{display:flex;gap:4px;margin-left:auto}
  81        .fb button{background:none;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:2px 7px;cursor:pointer;font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted)}
  82        .fb button.on{background:var(--green-bg);border-color:var(--green);color:var(--green-dark)}
  83        .more{align-self:center}
  84        .typing{display:flex;gap:4px;padding:6px 14px}
  85        .typing span{width:7px;height:7px;background:var(--green);border-radius:50%;opacity:.4;animation:t 1.2s infinite}
  86        .typing span:nth-child(2){animation-delay:.2s}.typing span:nth-child(3){animation-delay:.4s}
  87        @keyframes t{0%,100%{transform:translateY(0);opacity:.4}50%{transform:translateY(-4px);opacity:1}}
  88        /* composer */
  89        .composer{display:flex;gap:8px;padding:10px 14px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--card);padding-bottom:calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))}
  90        .composer textarea{resize:none;max-height:120px;line-height:1.4;padding:11px 13px}
  91        .send{width:48px;height:48px;border-radius:8px;background:var(--green);color:#fff;border:none;cursor:pointer;flex-shrink:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center}
  92        .send:hover{background:var(--green-dark)}
  93        /* modal */
  94        .modal{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(15,23,42,.45);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px;z-index:50}

  96        .modal h3{margin-bottom:12px}
  97        .modal .field{margin-bottom:12px}

 178                <div class="field"><label>Mkoa wako (kwa huduma zilizo karibu)</label><select name="region" id="p-region"><option value="">— Chagua mkoa —</option></select></div>
