 185<script>
 186(() => {
 187    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
 188    // The session (and therefore the CSRF token) is regenerated on login and logout,
 189    // so the token is kept in a variable and refreshed from server responses.
 190    let csrf = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content;
 191    const refreshCsrf = async () => {
 192        try { const r = await fetch('/chat/session', { headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' } }); const d = await r.json(); if (d.csrf) csrf = d.csrf; } catch (e) {}
 193    };
 194    const state = { user: null, settings: null, phone: '', lang: 'sw', oldest: null, hasMore: false, filtering: false };
 195    const SUGGESTIONS = {
 196        sw: ['Nifanyeje kupata kitambulisho cha NIDA?', 'Haki zangu ni zipi nikikamatwa na polisi?', 'Dalili za malaria kwa mtoto ni zipi?', 'Nipande mahindi lini mkoa wangu?', 'Nieleze fractions kwa darasa la sita', 'Nianzeje biashara ndogo?'],
 197        en: ['How do I register for a NIDA ID?', 'What are my rights if the police arrest me?', 'What are malaria danger signs in a child?', 'When should I plant maize in my region?', 'Explain fractions for Standard Six', 'How do I start a small business?']
 198    };
 199
 200    const api = async (url, opts = {}, retried = false) => {
 201        const res = await fetch(url, Object.assign({ headers: Object.assign({ 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest', 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrf, 'Accept': 'application/json' }, opts.body && !(opts.body instanceof FormData) ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}) }, opts));
 202        let data = {};
 203        try { data = await res.json(); } catch (e) {}
 204        if (res.status === 419 && !retried) { await refreshCsrf(); return api(url, opts, true); }
 205        if (data && data.csrf) csrf = data.csrf;
 206        if (!res.ok) { const msg = data.message || (data.errors && Object.values(data.errors)[0][0]) || ('Kosa ' + res.status); throw new Error(msg); }
 207        return data;
 208    };
 209    const show = (id) => { document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active')); $(id).classList.add('active'); };
 210    const err = (el, msg) => { el.textContent = msg; el.classList.remove('hidden'); };
 211    const hide = (el) => el.classList.add('hidden');
 212    const t = (sw, en) => (state.lang === 'en' ? en : sw);
 213
 214    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- boot
 215    async function boot() {
 216        try {
 217            const s = await api('/chat/session');
 218            state.settings = s.settings;
 219            fillCategories(s.settings.categories);
 220            fillRegions(s.settings.regions);
 221            if (s.authenticated) { state.user = s.user; enterChat(); } else { show('auth-screen'); }
 222        } catch (e) { show('auth-screen'); }
 223    }
 224
 225    function fillCategories(cats) {
 226        const sel = $('f-category');
 227        cats.forEach(c => { const o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = c.key; o.textContent = c.sw + ' / ' + c.en; sel.appendChild(o); });
 228    }
 229    function fillRegions(regions) {
 230        const sel = $('p-region');
 231        regions.forEach(r => { const o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = r; o.textContent = r; sel.appendChild(o); });
 232    }
 233
 234    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- auth
 235    $('phone-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
 236        e.preventDefault();
 237        hide($('auth-error')); hide($('auth-ok'));
 238        const btn = $('phone-btn'); btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = '...';
 239        state.phone = $('phone').value.trim(); state.lang = $('lang').value;
 240        try {
 241            const r = await api('/chat/request-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone_number: state.phone, language: state.lang }) });
 242            if (!r.otp_required) { state.user = r.user; enterChat(); return; }
 243            $('phone-form').classList.add('hidden'); $('otp-form').classList.remove('hidden');
 244            let msg = t('Tumetuma namba ya uthibitisho kwa ' + r.phone_masked + '.', 'We sent a verification code to ' + r.phone_masked + '.');
 245            if (r.debug_code) msg += ' [dev: ' + r.debug_code + ']';
 246            $('auth-ok').textContent = msg; $('auth-ok').classList.remove('hidden');
 247            $('code').focus();
 248        } catch (ex) { err($('auth-error'), ex.message); }
 249        finally { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Endelea / Continue'; }
 250    });
 251    $('otp-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
 252        e.preventDefault();
 253        hide($('auth-error'));
 254        const btn = $('otp-btn'); btn.disabled = true;
 255        try {
 256            const r = await api('/chat/verify-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone_number: state.phone, code: $('code').value.trim() }) });
 257            state.user = r.user; enterChat();
 258        } catch (ex) { err($('auth-error'), ex.message); }
 259        finally { btn.disabled = false; }
 260    });
 261    $('otp-back').addEventListener('click', () => { $('otp-form').classList.add('hidden'); $('phone-form').classList.remove('hidden'); hide($('auth-ok')); });
 262    $('otp-resend').addEventListener('click', () => $('phone-form').requestSubmit());
 263
 264    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- chat
 265    async function enterChat() {
 266        const redirect = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('redirect');
 267        if (redirect && redirect.startsWith('/')) { location.href = redirect; return; }
 268        show('chat-screen');
 269        state.lang = state.user.preferred_language === 'en' ? 'en' : state.lang;
 270        $('who-line').textContent = (state.user.name ? state.user.name + ' · ' : '') + state.user.phone_masked + (state.user.region ? ' · ' + state.user.region : '');
 271        await loadMessages();
 272        const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('q');
 273        if (q) { $('input').value = q; $('input').focus(); history.replaceState({}, '', '/chat'); }
 274    }
 275
 276    async function loadMessages(filters = {}, append = false) {
 277        const params = new URLSearchParams(filters);
 278        const r = await api('/chat/messages?' + params.toString());
 279        const box = $('msgs');
 280        if (!append) box.innerHTML = '';
 281        if (!append && r.messages.length === 0 && !state.filtering) renderWelcome();
 282        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
 283        r.messages.forEach(m => frag.appendChild(renderMsg(m)));
 284        if (append) { box.querySelector('.more')?.remove(); box.prepend(frag); } else { box.appendChild(frag); }
 285        state.hasMore = r.has_more; state.oldest = r.messages.length ? r.messages[0].id : state.oldest;
 286        if (state.hasMore && !state.filtering) {
 287            const b = document.createElement('button'); b.className = 'ibtn more'; b.textContent = t('Onyesha mazungumzo ya zamani', 'Show older messages');
 288            b.onclick = () => loadMessages({ before: state.oldest }, true); box.prepend(b);
 289        }
 290        if (!append) scrollBottom();
 291    }
 292
 293    function renderWelcome() {
 294        const w = document.createElement('div'); w.className = 'welcome';
 295        w.innerHTML = '<h3>' + t('Karibu! Uliza lolote kuhusu maisha Tanzania.', 'Welcome! Ask anything about life in Tanzania.') + '</h3><p>' + t('Sheria, afya, kilimo, masomo, huduma za serikali, fedha, ajira, teknolojia... Taja mahali ulipo ukihitaji huduma iliyo karibu.', 'Law, health, farming, school subjects, government services, money, jobs, technology... Mention where you are if you need nearby services.') + '</p><div class="sugg"></div>';
 296        const s = w.querySelector('.sugg');
 297        SUGGESTIONS[state.lang].forEach(q => { const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = q; b.onclick = () => { $('input').value = q; $('composer').requestSubmit(); }; s.appendChild(b); });
 298        $('msgs').appendChild(w);
 299    }
 300
 301    function renderMsg(m) {
 302        const d = document.createElement('div');
 303        const isIn = m.direction === 'inbound';
 304        d.className = 'msg ' + (isIn ? 'in' : 'out');
 305        d.dataset.id = m.id || '';
 306        const body = document.createElement('div'); body.textContent = m.content; d.appendChild(body);
 307        const meta = document.createElement('div'); meta.className = 'meta';
 308        const time = document.createElement('span'); time.textContent = m.created_at ? new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleString('sw-TZ', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', month: 'short' }) : '';
 309        meta.appendChild(time);
 310        if (!isIn && m.category_label) { const c = document.createElement('span'); c.className = 'cat'; c.textContent = m.category_label; meta.appendChild(c); }
 311        if (!isIn && m.id) {
 312            const fb = document.createElement('div'); fb.className = 'fb';
 313            [[1, '👍'], [-1, '👎']].forEach(([v, icon]) => {
 314                const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = icon; b.title = v === 1 ? t('Imesaidia', 'Helpful') : t('Haikusaidia', 'Not helpful');
 315                if (m.feedback === v) b.classList.add('on');
 316                b.onclick = async () => { try { await api('/chat/feedback', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ message_id: m.id, rating: v }) }); fb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on')); b.classList.add('on'); } catch (e) {} };
 317                fb.appendChild(b);
 318            });
 319            meta.appendChild(fb);
 320        }
 321        d.appendChild(meta);
 322        return d;
 323    }
 324
 325    function addSystem(text) { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'msg sys'; d.textContent = text; $('msgs').appendChild(d); scrollBottom(); }
 326    function scrollBottom() { const b = $('msgs'); b.scrollTop = b.scrollHeight; }
 327
 328    $('composer').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
 329        e.preventDefault();
 330        const text = $('input').value.trim();
 331        if (!text) return;
 332        $('msgs').querySelector('.welcome')?.remove();
 333        $('msgs').appendChild(renderMsg({ direction: 'inbound', content: text, created_at: new Date().toISOString() }));
 334        $('input').value = ''; $('input').style.height = 'auto';
 335        $('typing').classList.remove('hidden'); scrollBottom();
 336        try {
 337            const r = await api('/chat/send', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ message: text }) });
 338            $('typing').classList.add('hidden');
 339            if (r.reply) $('msgs').appendChild(renderMsg(r.reply));
 340        } catch (ex) {
 341            $('typing').classList.add('hidden');
 342            if (/401/.test(ex.message)) { state.user = null; show('auth-screen'); return; }
 343            addSystem('⚠️ ' + ex.message);
 344        }
 345        scrollBottom();
 346    });
 347    $('input').addEventListener('input', function () { this.style.height = 'auto'; this.style.height = Math.min(this.scrollHeight, 120) + 'px'; });
 348    $('input').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); $('composer').requestSubmit(); } });
 349
 350    // filters
 351    $('f-apply').addEventListener('click', () => {
 352        const f = { keyword: $('f-keyword').value.trim(), date: $('f-date').value, category: $('f-category').value };
 353        Object.keys(f).forEach(k => { if (!f[k]) delete f[k]; });
 354        if (!Object.keys(f).length) return;
 355        state.filtering = true; $('f-reset').classList.remove('hidden'); $('f-status').classList.remove('hidden');
 356        $('f-status').textContent = t('Matokeo ya utafutaji', 'Search results');
 357        loadMessages(f);
 358    });
 359    $('f-reset').addEventListener('click', () => { $('f-keyword').value = ''; $('f-date').value = ''; $('f-category').value = ''; state.filtering = false; hide($('f-reset')); hide($('f-status')); loadMessages(); });
 360
 361    // prefs
 362    $('prefs-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { $('p-name').value = state.user.name || ''; $('p-lang').value = state.user.preferred_language || 'auto'; $('p-region').value = state.user.region || ''; $('prefs').classList.remove('hidden'); });
 363    $('prefs-cancel').addEventListener('click', () => hide($('prefs')));
 364    $('prefs-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
 365        e.preventDefault();
 366        try {
 367            const r = await api('/chat/preferences', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ name: $('p-name').value, preferred_language: $('p-lang').value, region: $('p-region').value }) });
 368            state.user = r.user; hide($('prefs'));
 369            state.lang = state.user.preferred_language === 'en' ? 'en' : (state.user.preferred_language === 'sw' ? 'sw' : state.lang);
 370            $('who-line').textContent = (state.user.name ? state.user.name + ' · ' : '') + state.user.phone_masked + (state.user.region ? ' · ' + state.user.region : '');
 371            addSystem(t('Mipangilio imehifadhiwa.', 'Preferences saved.'));
 372        } catch (ex) { alert(ex.message); }
 373    });
 374
 375    $('logout-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
 376        if (!confirm(t('Unataka kutoka?', 'Sign out?'))) return;
 377        await api('/chat/logout', { method: 'POST' }); state.user = null; $('msgs').innerHTML = ''; show('auth-screen');
 378    });
 379
 380    // offline awareness + PWA
 381    const net = () => $('offline-bar').classList.toggle('hidden', navigator.onLine);
 382    window.addEventListener('online', net); window.addEventListener('offline', net); net();
 383    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(() => {});
 384
 385    boot();
 386})();
 387</script>
 388</body>
 389</html>

   1@extends('layouts.public', ['title' => 'Sera ya Faragha', 'description' => 'Jinsi Huru SMS inavyokusanya, kutumia na kulinda taarifa zako.'])
   2
   3@section('content')
   4<h1>Sera ya Faragha · Privacy Policy</h1>
   5<p class="updated">Ilisasishwa: {{ date('d F Y') }}</p>
   6
   7<h2>1. Taarifa tunazokusanya</h2>
   8<ul>
   9    <li><strong>Namba ya simu</strong> – ndiyo kitambulisho chako cha huduma, kwa SMS na web chat.</li>
  10    <li><strong>Maswali na majibu</strong> – tunahifadhi mazungumzo ili kuendeleza muktadha wa maswali ya kufuatilia na kukuwezesha kutafuta majibu ya zamani.</li>
  11    <li><strong>Mapendeleo</strong> – jina (ukilitoa), lugha unayopendelea, mkoa (ukiutaja) na maoni yako kuhusu majibu.</li>
  12    <li><strong>Taarifa za kiufundi</strong> – muda wa ujumbe, njia uliyotumia (SMS au web) na kumbukumbu za mfumo kwa usalama.</li>
  13</ul>
  14
  15<h2>2. Jinsi tunavyotumia taarifa</h2>
  16<p>Kutoa majibu, kuendeleza mazungumzo, kulinda huduma dhidi ya matumizi mabaya, kuboresha maarifa yaliyohifadhiwa na kutoa takwimu za jumla (bila kukutambulisha) kwa washirika. Maswali yako hutumwa kwa mtoa huduma wa teknolojia ya lugha ili kupata jibu; hatutumi jina lako wala namba yako ya simu pamoja na swali.</p>
  17<p><em>We use your data to answer you, keep conversation context, protect the service from abuse and produce anonymous statistics. Questions are sent to a language technology provider to generate answers; your name and phone number are not sent with the question.</em></p>
  18
  19<h2>3. Kushirikisha taarifa</h2>
  20<p>Hatuuzi taarifa zako. Tunaweza kushirikisha taarifa pale sheria inapotaka au kwa watoa huduma wanaotuwezesha kutoa huduma (mtandao wa SMS, mwenyeji wa seva, mtoa huduma wa teknolojia ya lugha) chini ya masharti ya usiri.</p>
  21
  22<h2>4. Uhifadhi na usalama</h2>
  23<p>Taarifa huhifadhiwa kwenye seva zilizolindwa. Namba ya uthibitisho (OTP) huhifadhiwa kwa njia ya siri na hufutwa baada ya kutumika. Tunahifadhi mazungumzo kwa muda unaohitajika kwa huduma; unaweza kuomba kufutwa.</p>
  24
  25<h2>5. Haki zako</h2>
  26<ul>
  27    <li>Kuona mazungumzo yako kupitia web chat.</li>
  28    <li>Kusitisha majibu wakati wowote kwa kutuma <strong>ACHA</strong> kwa SMS, na kuendelea kwa <strong>ANZA</strong>.</li>
  29    <li>Kuomba kufutwa kwa taarifa zako kwa kutumia fomu ya mawasiliano.</li>
  30</ul>
  31
  32<h2>6. Watoto</h2>
  33<p>Huduma inaweza kutumiwa na wanafunzi kwa maswali ya masomo. Hatukusanyi kwa makusudi taarifa za ziada za watoto zaidi ya zile zilizo muhimu kwa huduma.</p>
  34
  35<h2>7. Mawasiliano</h2>
  36<p>Huru Digital Co. Ltd., Tanzania. Tumia fomu kwenye <a href="{{ route('welcome') }}#wasiliana">ukurasa wa mwanzo</a>.</p>
  37@endsection

  12<p>Majibu ni maelezo ya jumla yanayotolewa kiotomatiki kwa msaada wa teknolojia na maarifa yaliyokusanywa. Si ushauri wa kisheria, wa kitabibu, wa kifedha wala uwakilishi wa aina yoyote. Kwa maamuzi muhimu, thibitisha na ofisi, mtaalamu au mamlaka husika. Kwa dharura piga 112 (polisi), 114 (zimamoto), 115 (gari la wagonjwa) au 116 (msaada kwa mtoto).</p>

  25<h2>5. Taarifa zako</h2>
  26<p>Tunahifadhi namba yako ya simu, maswali na majibu ili kuendeleza mazungumzo na kuboresha huduma. Soma <a href="{{ route('legal.privacy') }}">Sera ya Faragha</a> kwa maelezo kamili. Tuma ACHA kwa SMS kusitisha majibu wakati wowote.</p>

105 tests/Feature/CommunityTest.php 45 lines

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace Tests\Feature;
   4
   5use App\Models\CommunityThread;
   6use App\Models\User;
   7use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
   8use Tests\TestCase;
   9
  10class CommunityTest extends TestCase
  11{
  12    use RefreshDatabase;
  13
  14    public function test_guest_is_redirected_to_chat(): void
  15    {
  16        $this->get(route('community.index'))->assertRedirect(route('chat.index', ['redirect' => '/community']));
  17    }
  18
  19    public function test_user_can_create_join_and_post(): void
  20    {
  21        $user = User::factory()->create();
  22        $session = ['chat_user_id' => $user->id];
  23
  24        $this->withSession($session)->post(route('community.threads.store'), ['title' => 'Bei za mahindi', 'description' => 'Dodoma'])->assertRedirect();
  25        $thread = CommunityThread::where('title', 'Bei za mahindi')->firstOrFail();
  26        $this->assertTrue($thread->members()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists());
  27
  28        $other = User::factory()->create();
  29        $this->withSession(['chat_user_id' => $other->id])->post(route('community.join', $thread->slug))->assertRedirect(route('community.show', $thread));
  30        $this->withSession(['chat_user_id' => $other->id])->post(route('community.posts.store', $thread->slug), ['content' => 'Shilingi 800 kwa kilo Kongwa'])->assertRedirect();
  31
  32        $this->assertDatabaseHas('community_posts', ['content' => 'Shilingi 800 kwa kilo Kongwa', 'is_approved' => true]);
  33        $this->withSession($session)->get(route('community.show', $thread->slug))->assertOk()->assertSee('Shilingi 800');
  34    }
  35
  36    public function test_abusive_posts_are_rejected(): void
  37    {
  38        $user = User::factory()->create();
  39        $thread = CommunityThread::create(['title' => 'T', 'slug' => 't', 'is_private' => false]);
  40        $thread->members()->attach($user->id);
  41
  42        $this->withSession(['chat_user_id' => $user->id])->postJson(route('community.posts.store', $thread->slug), ['content' => 'wewe mjinga sana'])->assertStatus(422);
  43        $this->assertSame(1, $user->fresh()->abuse_count);
  44    }
  45}

  21    public function test_inbound_webhook_rejects_bad_token_when_secret_is_set(): void

  14class WebChatTest extends TestCase

  17
  18    public function test_login_without_otp_when_sms_is_not_configured_locally(): void
  19    {
  20        $this->mock(SmsService::class, fn (MockInterface $m) => $m->shouldReceive('isConfigured')->andReturn(false));
  21
  22        $login = $this->postJson('/chat/request-otp', ['phone_number' => '0712345678'])
  23            ->assertOk()
  24            ->assertJsonPath('otp_required', false)
  25            ->assertJsonPath('user.phone_masked', '+255 *** 678');
  26
  27        // Login regenerates the session; the rotated CSRF token must be handed back.
  28        $this->assertNotEmpty($login->json('csrf'));
  29        $this->assertSame($login->json('csrf'), $this->getJson('/chat/session')->assertJsonPath('authenticated', true)->json('csrf'));
  30    }
  31
  32    public function test_otp_flow_when_sms_is_configured(): void
  33    {
  34        Settings::set('otp_enabled', '1');
  35        $this->mock(SmsService::class, function (MockInterface $m) {
  36            $m->shouldReceive('isConfigured')->andReturn(true);
  37            $m->shouldReceive('sendOtp')->once()->andReturn(['status' => 'success']);
  38        });
  39
  40        $r = $this->postJson('/chat/request-otp', ['phone_number' => '0712345678'])->assertOk()->assertJsonPath('otp_required', true);
  41        $code = $r->json('debug_code');
  42        $this->assertNotEmpty($code, 'debug code is exposed in testing');
  43
  44        $this->getJson('/chat/session')->assertJsonPath('authenticated', false);
  45        $this->postJson('/chat/verify-otp', ['phone_number' => '0712345678', 'code' => '000000'])->assertStatus(422);
  46        $this->postJson('/chat/verify-otp', ['phone_number' => '0712345678', 'code' => $code])->assertOk();
  47        $this->getJson('/chat/session')->assertJsonPath('authenticated', true);
  48
  49        $this->assertNull(User::first()->otp_hash);
  50    }
  51
  52    public function test_messages_require_a_session_and_send_returns_reply(): void
  53    {
  54        $this->getJson('/chat/messages')->assertStatus(401);
  55
  56        $this->mock(LlmClient::class, function (MockInterface $mock) {
  57            $mock->shouldReceive('isConfigured')->andReturn(true);
  58            $mock->shouldReceive('generate')->once()->andReturn([
  59                'status' => LlmClient::STATUS_OK, 'text' => 'Malaria danger signs include convulsions.',
  60                'model' => 'gemini-test', 'tokens' => ['prompt' => 1, 'completion' => 1, 'total' => 2], 'latency_ms' => 50, 'finish_reason' => 'STOP', 'error' => null,
  61            ]);
  62        });
  63
  64        $user = User::factory()->create(['phone_number' => '+255712345678']);
  65
  66        $this->withSession(['chat_user_id' => $user->id])
  67            ->postJson('/chat/send', ['message' => 'What are malaria danger signs in a child?'])
  68            ->assertOk()
  69            ->assertJsonPath('kind', 'answer')
  70            ->assertJsonPath('category', 'health')
  71            ->assertJsonPath('reply.content', 'Malaria danger signs include convulsions.');
  72
  73        $replyId = $this->withSession(['chat_user_id' => $user->id])->getJson('/chat/messages')->json('messages.1.id');
  74
  75        $this->withSession(['chat_user_id' => $user->id])
  76            ->postJson('/chat/feedback', ['message_id' => $replyId, 'rating' => 1])
  77            ->assertOk();
  78
  79        $this->assertDatabaseHas('feedback', ['message_id' => $replyId, 'rating' => 1]);
  80    }
  81
  82    public function test_admin_area_requires_an_admin_account(): void
  83    {
  84        $this->get('/admin')->assertRedirect('/admin/login');
  85
  86        $citizen = User::factory()->create(['email' => 'c@example.com', 'password' => Hash::make('secret123')]);
  87        $this->actingAs($citizen)->get('/admin')->assertStatus(403);
  88
  89        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create(['email' => 'a@example.com', 'password' => Hash::make('secret123')]);
  90        $this->post('/admin/login', ['email' => 'a@example.com', 'password' => 'secret123'])->assertRedirect('/admin');
  91        $this->actingAs($admin)->get('/admin')->assertOk()->assertSee('Dashboard');
  92        $this->actingAs($admin)->get('/admin/settings')->assertOk()->assertSee('Gemini model');
  93    }
  94
  95    public function test_settings_only_accept_known_keys(): void
  96    {
  97        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();
  98
  99        $this->actingAs($admin)->post('/admin/settings', ['app_name' => 'Huru', 'bogus_key' => 'x'] + $this->defaults())->assertRedirect();
 100        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('system_settings', ['key' => 'bogus_key']);
 101        $this->assertSame('Huru', Settings::get('app_name'));
 102    }
 103
 104    private function defaults(): array
 105    {
 106        $out = [];
 107        foreach (Settings::schema() as $k => $def) {
 108            $out[$k] = $def['default'];
 109        }
 110
 111        return $out;
 112    }

   1<?php
   2
   3namespace Tests\Unit;
   4
   5use App\Services\LanguageDetector;
   6use App\Services\TanzaniaContext;
   7use App\Support\Phone;
   8use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
   9
  10class LanguageAndLocationTest extends TestCase
  11{
  12    public function test_language_detection(): void
  13    {
  14        $d = new LanguageDetector();
  15        $this->assertSame('sw', $d->detect('Naomba kujua haki zangu nikikamatwa'));
  16        $this->assertSame('en', $d->detect('What are my rights when arrested?'));
  17        $this->assertSame('sw', $d->detect('nimedhulumiwa shamba langu kijijini'));
  18        $this->assertNull($d->detect(''));
  19    }
  20
  21    public function test_location_detection(): void
  22    {
  23        $tz = new TanzaniaContext();
  24
  25        $loc = $tz->detectLocation('naomba msaada wa kisheria nipo Sinza Dar es Salaam');
  26        $this->assertSame('dar es salaam', $loc['region']);
  27        $this->assertSame('ubungo', $loc['district']);
  28        $this->assertSame('sinza', $loc['area']);
  29
  30        $loc = $tz->detectLocation('Nipo Kahama Shinyanga');
  31        $this->assertSame('shinyanga', $loc['region']);
  32        $this->assertSame('kahama', $loc['district']);
  33
  34        $loc = $tz->detectLocation('I live in Mwanza');
  35        $this->assertSame('mwanza', $loc['region']);
  36
  37        $this->assertNull($tz->detectLocation('Hakuna mahali hapa'));
  38    }
  39
  40    public function test_phone_normalisation(): void
  41    {
  42        $this->assertSame('+255712345678', Phone::normalize('0712 345 678'));
  43        $this->assertSame('+255712345678', Phone::normalize('255712345678'));
  44        $this->assertSame('+255712345678', Phone::normalize('+255712345678'));
  45        $this->assertSame('+255712345678', Phone::normalize('712345678'));
  46        $this->assertSame('+254700000000', Phone::normalize('+254700000000'));
  47        $this->assertNull(Phone::normalize('abc'));
  48        $this->assertTrue(Phone::isTanzanian('+255712345678'));
  49        $this->assertFalse(Phone::isTanzanian('+254700000000'));
  50    }
  51
  52    public function test_locale_block_mentions_tanzania_and_currency(): void
  53    {
  54        $tz = new TanzaniaContext();
  55        $this->assertStringContainsString('Tanzania', $tz->localeBlock('en'));
  56        $this->assertStringContainsString('TZS', $tz->localeBlock('sw'));
  57        $this->assertStringContainsString('112', $tz->localeBlock('sw'));
  58    }
  59}

   2
   3namespace Tests\Unit;

   5use App\Models\KnowledgeEntry;

  13class PromptEngineTest extends TestCase

  16
  17    public function test_it_injects_matching_knowledge_and_country_context(): void
  18    {
  19        PromptTemplate::create(['name' => 'Legal SW', 'category' => 'legal', 'language' => 'sw', 'template' => 'Wewe ni {app_name}, mwelimishaji wa sheria.', 'is_active' => true]);
  20        KnowledgeEntry::create([
  21            'title' => 'Haki zako ukikamatwa na polisi',
  22            'content' => 'Polisi wanapaswa kukufikisha mahakamani ndani ya saa 24.',
  23            'category' => 'legal', 'language' => 'sw', 'keywords' => ['kukamatwa', 'polisi', 'dhamana'], 'is_active' => true,
  24        ]);
  25
  26        $built = app(PromptEngine::class)->build([
  27            'question' => 'Nimekamatwa na polisi, haki zangu ni zipi?',
  28            'language' => 'sw', 'category' => 'legal', 'channel' => 'sms',
  29        ]);
  30
  31        $this->assertStringContainsString('saa 24', $built['system']);
  32        $this->assertStringContainsString('mwelimishaji wa sheria', $built['system']);
  33        $this->assertStringContainsString('Tanzania', $built['system']);
  34        $this->assertStringContainsString('TZS', $built['system']);
  35        $this->assertSame('user', $built['turns'][0]['role']);
  36        $this->assertCount(1, $built['knowledge']);
  37    }
  38
  39    public function test_it_adds_nearby_services_when_location_is_mentioned(): void
  40    {
  41        LocalResource::create(['name' => 'KAHAMA PARALEGAL AID ORGANIZATION', 'category' => 'legal_aid', 'region' => 'shinyanga', 'district' => 'kahama', 'location' => 'Kahama mjini', 'phone' => '+255 767 994457', 'is_active' => true]);
  42
  43        $built = app(PromptEngine::class)->build([
  44            'question' => 'naomba msaada wa kisheria nipo kahama shinyanga',
  45            'language' => 'sw', 'category' => 'legal', 'channel' => 'web',
  46            'location' => ['region' => 'shinyanga', 'district' => 'kahama', 'area' => null],
  47        ]);
  48
  49        $this->assertStringContainsString('KAHAMA PARALEGAL AID ORGANIZATION', $built['system']);
  50        $this->assertStringContainsString('+255 767 994457', $built['system']);
  51    }
  52
  53    public function test_emergency_puts_emergency_contacts_in_prompt_and_history_is_kept(): void
  54    {
  55        ReferenceContact::create(['name' => 'Ambulance', 'category' => 'health', 'phone' => '115', 'is_emergency' => true, 'is_active' => true]);
  56
  57        $built = app(PromptEngine::class)->build([
  58            'question' => 'My father collapsed and is not breathing',
  59            'language' => 'en', 'category' => 'health', 'channel' => 'web', 'is_emergency' => true,
  60            'history' => [['role' => 'user', 'text' => 'hello'], ['role' => 'model', 'text' => 'hi']],
  61        ]);
  62
  63        $this->assertStringContainsString('115', $built['system']);
  64        $this->assertStringContainsString('EMERGENCY', $built['system']);
  65        $this->assertCount(3, $built['turns']);
  66    }
  67}

   1<?php

   3namespace Tests\Unit;

   5use App\Services\TopicClassifier;

   7
   8class TopicClassifierTest extends TestCase
   9{

  13    {

  18    public function test_detects_legal_questions_in_swahili(): void
  19    {

  21        $this->assertSame('legal', $r['category']);
  22        $this->assertFalse($r['is_emergency']);
  23    }
  24
  25    public function test_detects_health_questions_in_english(): void
  26    {
  27        $r = $this->classifier->classify('My child has fever and vomiting, is it malaria?');
  28        $this->assertSame('health', $r['category']);
  29    }
  30
  31    public function test_detects_agriculture(): void
  32    {
  33        $r = $this->classifier->classify('Nipande mahindi lini na nitumie mbolea gani?');
  34        $this->assertSame('agriculture', $r['category']);
  35    }
  36
  37    public function test_detects_government_services(): void
  38    {
  39        $r = $this->classifier->classify('How do I get a NIDA national id and a passport?');
  40        $this->assertSame('government', $r['category']);
  41    }
  42
  43    public function test_falls_back_to_general(): void
  44    {
  45        $r = $this->classifier->classify('Habari za asubuhi');
  46        $this->assertSame('general', $r['category']);
  47    }
  48
  49    public function test_flags_emergencies(): void
  50    {
  51        $r = $this->classifier->classify('Dharura! mtoto amezimia na hapumui');
  52        $this->assertTrue($r['is_emergency']);
  53        $this->assertSame('health', $r['category']);
  54    }
  55
  56    public function test_labels_exist_for_every_category(): void
  57    {
  58        foreach (TopicClassifier::keys() as $key) {
  59            $this->assertNotEmpty(TopicClassifier::label($key, 'sw'));
  60            $this->assertNotEmpty(TopicClassifier::label($key, 'en'));
  61        }
  62    }
  63}
